            end
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end

local function setNetworkOwner(part)
    if not part then return end
    local grabEvents = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
    if not grabEvents then return end
    local setNet = grabEvents:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")
    if not setNet then return end
    pcall(function() setNet:FireServer(part, part.CFrame) end)
end
local function destroyGrabLine(part)
    if not part then return end
    local grabEvents = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
    if not grabEvents then return end
    local destroyLine = grabEvents:FindFirstChild("DestroyGrabLine")
    if not destroyLine then return end
    pcall(function() destroyLine:FireServer(part) end)
end
local function destroyBanana(banana)
    if not banana then return end
    local menuToys = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
    if not menuToys then return end
    local destroyToy = menuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")
    if not destroyToy then return end
    pcall(function() destroyToy:FireServer(banana) end)
end
local function spawnBanana()
    local hrp = getHumanoidRootPart()
    if not hrp then return nil end
    local spawnPos = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
    local menuToys = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
    if not menuToys then return nil end
    local spawnRemote = menuToys:FindFirstChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")
    if not spawnRemote then return nil end
    local spawned = nil
    local folder = getSpawnedToysFolder()
    if not folder then return nil end
    local conn
    conn = folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "FoodBanana" then spawned = child; conn:Disconnect() end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() spawnRemote:InvokeServer("FoodBanana", spawnPos, Vector3.zero) end)
    end)
    local start = tick()
    repeat task.wait(0.05) until spawned or (tick() - start) > 3
    if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
    return spawned
end
local function holdBanana(banana)
    if not banana then return end
    local holdPart = banana:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
    if not holdPart then return end
    local holdRemote = holdPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
    if not holdRemote then return end
    local char = getLocalChar()
    if not char then return end
    local success = false
    for i = 1, 5 do
        if not banana or not banana.Parent then break end
        local result = pcall(function() return holdRemote:InvokeServer(banana, char) end)
        if result then success = true; break end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    return success
end
local function useBanana(banana)
    if not banana then return end
    local useEvent = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("HoldEvents") and replicatedStorage.HoldEvents:FindFirstChild("Use")
    if not useEvent then return end
    pcall(function() useEvent:FireServer(banana) end)
end
local function dropBanana(banana)
    if not banana then return end
    local holdPart = banana:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
    if not holdPart then return end
    local dropRemote = holdPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
    if not dropRemote then return end
    local hrp = getHumanoidRootPart()
    if not hrp then return end
    local dropPos = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 15, 0)
    pcall(function() dropRemote:InvokeServer(banana, dropPos, dropPos) end)
end
local function getTargetLeg()
    if not selectedTargetPlayerObj then return nil end
    if not selectedTargetPlayerObj.Character then return nil end
    return selectedTargetPlayerObj.Character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
end
local function getTargetHealth()
    if not selectedTargetPlayerObj then return 0 end
    if not selectedTargetPlayerObj.Character then return 0 end
    local hum = selectedTargetPlayerObj.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return 0 end
    return hum.Health
end
local function fullBananaCycle()
    if not selectedTargetPlayerObj then return false end
    local banana = spawnBanana()
    if not banana then return false end
    holdBanana(banana)
    task.wait(0.05)
    holdBanana(banana)
    task.wait(0.05)
    task.wait(0.5)
    useBanana(banana)
    task.wait(2.3)
    dropBanana(banana)
    task.wait(0.05)
    dropBanana(banana)
    task.wait(0.3)
    return banana
end
local function restartLoop(banana)
    if loopConnection then loopConnection:Disconnect(); loopConnection = nil end
    currentBanana = banana
    local hitboxPart = banana:FindFirstChild("HitboxPart")
    if not hitboxPart then return end
    loopConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not isLooping then stopLoopLocal(); return end
        if not currentBanana or not currentBanana.Parent then return end
        local folder = getSpawnedToysFolder()
        local bananaExists = false
        if folder then
            for _, child in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name == "FoodBanana" then
                    bananaExists = true
                    currentBanana = child
                    hitboxPart = child:FindFirstChild("HitboxPart")
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not bananaExists or not hitboxPart then
            loopConnection:Disconnect(); loopConnection = nil; currentBanana = nil
            return
        end
        local hrp = getHumanoidRootPart()
        if not hrp then return end
        local targetPos, targetCFrame
        local health = getTargetHealth()
        if health <= 0 then
            targetPos = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 7, 0)
            targetCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos.Position)
        else
            local leg = getTargetLeg()
            if leg then
                targetPos = leg.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0)
                targetCFrame = leg.CFrame
            else
                targetPos = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 7, 0)
                targetCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos.Position)
            end
        end
        setNetworkOwner(hitboxPart)
        destroyGrabLine(hitboxPart)
        local bp = hitboxPart:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")
        if bp then bp.Position = targetPos.Position
        else
            bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
            bp.P = 10000; bp.D = 100
            bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
            bp.Position = targetPos.Position
            bp.Parent = hitboxPart
        end
        local bg = hitboxPart:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")
        if bg then bg.CFrame = targetCFrame
        else
            bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            bg.P = 10000; bg.D = 100
            bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
            bg.CFrame = targetCFrame
            bg.Parent = hitboxPart
        end
    end)
end
local function startLoopLocal()
    if isLooping then return end
    if not selectedTargetPlayerObj then return end
    isLooping = true
    local banana = fullBananaCycle()
    if banana then restartLoop(banana) end
    monitorConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not isLooping then return end
        local folder = getSpawnedToysFolder()
        local bananaExists = false
        if folder then
            for _, child in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name == "FoodBanana" then bananaExists = true; break end
            end
        end
        if not bananaExists then
            if loopConnection then loopConnection:Disconnect(); loopConnection = nil end
            task.wait(0.05)
            local newBanana = fullBananaCycle()
            if newBanana then restartLoop(newBanana) end
        end
    end)
end
function stopLoopLocal()
    isLooping = false
    if loopConnection then loopConnection:Disconnect(); loopConnection = nil end
    if monitorConnection then monitorConnection:Disconnect(); monitorConnection = nil end
    if currentBanana then
        destroyBanana(currentBanana)
        local hitboxPart = currentBanana:FindFirstChild("HitboxPart")
        if hitboxPart then
            local bp = hitboxPart:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition")
            if bp then bp:Destroy() end
            local bg = hitboxPart:FindFirstChild("BodyGyro")
            if bg then bg:Destroy() end
        end
        currentBanana = nil
    end
    local folder = getSpawnedToysFolder()
    if folder then
        for _, child in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name == "FoodBanana" then destroyBanana(child) end
        end
    end
end

RagdollGroup:AddToggle("RagdollKill", {
    Text = "Pen Kill",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            if not selectedTargetPlayerObj then
                Toggles.RagdollKill:SetValue(false)
                return
            end
            isRagdollActive = true
            doSticky()
            RagdollRespawnMonitor()
            ragdollMonitorTask = task.spawn(PencilMonitor)
        else
            isRagdollActive = false
            if ragdollMonitorTask then task.cancel(ragdollMonitorTask); ragdollMonitorTask = nil end
            destroyAllPencils()
        end
    end
})

RagdollGroup:AddToggle("LoopBananaRagdoll", {
    Text = "Loop Banana Ragdoll",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            if not selectedTargetPlayerObj then
                Toggles.LoopBananaRagdoll:SetValue(false)
                return
            end
            stopLoopLocal()
            startLoopLocal()
        else
            stopLoopLocal()
        end
    end
})
-- ============================================================
-- Kill Mode: ソースコードそのまま追加
-- ============================================================

-- ---------- Shared Helpers (キル.txt そのまま) ----------
local function GetHRH(c)
    if c then
        return c:FindFirstChild("Head"), c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    end
    return nil, nil, nil
end

local function Teleport(part, cf, offset)
    if part and cf and offset then
        part.CFrame = cf * offset
        part.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        part.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
end

local function Ownerboat(part)
    if not part then return nil end
    replicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
    if part:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and part.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
        return part.PartOwner
    end
    return nil
end

local function UnGrab(part)
    local dgl = replicatedStorage.GrabEvents:FindFirstChild("DestroyGrabLine")
    if part and dgl then dgl:FireServer(part) end
end

-- ---------- XOCU Kill (Loop Kill) ----------
local XOCU_HEIGHT_LIMIT = 100000
local XOCU_TELEPORT_OFFSET = Vector3.new(6, -18.5, 0)

local CameraAnchor = {}
CameraAnchor.__index = CameraAnchor
function CameraAnchor.new() return setmetatable({}, CameraAnchor) end
function CameraAnchor:attach(cf)
    self:detach()
    local p = Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name = "CameraAnchor"; p.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
    p.Transparency = 1; p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false
    p.CFrame = cf; p.Parent = Workspace; self.part = p
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom; cam.CameraSubject = p
end
function CameraAnchor:detach()
    if self.part then self.part:Destroy() self.part = nil end
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local c = LocalPlayer.Character
    if c and c:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
        cam.CameraSubject = c.Humanoid
    else
        cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
    end
end
local cameraAnchor = CameraAnchor.new()

local function XOCU_isTooHigh(plr)
    local c = plr.Character
    local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return not hrp or hrp.Position.Y > XOCU_HEIGHT_LIMIT
end
local function XOCU_setNoCollideChar(c)
    for _, v in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BasePart") then v.CanCollide = false end
    end
end
local function XOCU_saveOriginalPos()
    local c = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp then c:SetAttribute("OriginalPosition", hrp:GetPivot()) end
end
local function XOCU_getOriginalPos()
    local c = LocalPlayer.Character
    return c and c:GetAttribute("OriginalPosition") or nil
end
local function XOCU_scheduleReturnHome()
    local originalPos = XOCU_getOriginalPos()
    if not originalPos then return end
    local conn
    conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local c = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            hrp:PivotTo(originalPos)
            if getgenv().originalFallenHeight then
                Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = getgenv().originalFallenHeight
            end
            c:SetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos", false)
        end
        cameraAnchor:detach()
        conn:Disconnect()
    end)
end
local function XOCU_findBlobman()
    local toys = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    return toys and toys:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman") or nil
end
local function XOCU_ensureBlobman()
    local b = XOCU_findBlobman()
    if b then return b end
    replicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(
        "CreatureBlobman",
        LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5),
        Vector3.new(0, -15, 0)
    )
    for _ = 1, 30 do
        task.wait(0.1)
        b = XOCU_findBlobman()
        if b then return b end
    end
    return nil
end
local function XOCU_modifyTarget(root, hum)
    if not (root and hum) or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    local blob = XOCU_ensureBlobman()
    if blob and blob:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript") then
        local drop = blob.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
        if drop then
            for _, part in ipairs(hum.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("Weld") or part:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then
                    drop:FireServer(part, part)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    hum.Sit = false
    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
    hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
    local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(hum.Parent)
    if plr and plr:FindFirstChild("IsHeld") then plr.IsHeld.Value = false end
    local rag = hum:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
    if rag then rag.Value = false end
    local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    local bav = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
    bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e7, -1e7, 1e7); bv.P = 1e6
    bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 50), -50, math.random(-50, 50))
    bav.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(-1e7, -1e7, -1e7); bav.P = 1e6
    bav.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 300), math.random(-300, 300), math.random(-500, 500))
    bv.Parent = root; bav.Parent = root
    hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
    task.delay(2, function()
        if bv.Parent then bv:Destroy() end
        if bav.Parent then bav:Destroy() end
    end)
end

local LoopKillEnabled = false
local LoopKillHB = nil
RagdollGroup:AddDivider("Kill Mode 追加機能")
RagdollGroup:AddToggle("KillMode_XocuKill", {
    Text = "XOCU Kill",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        LoopKillEnabled = arg
        if arg then
            LoopKillHB = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not LoopKillEnabled or not selectedTargetPlayerObj then return end
                local target = selectedTargetPlayerObj
                local tChar = target and target.Character
                local tRoot = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local tHum = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                local tHead = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if not (target and tRoot and tHum and tHead) then return end
                if XOCU_isTooHigh(target) then return end
                if tHum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then return end
                local c = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not (c and hrp) then return end
                if not c:GetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos") then XOCU_saveOriginalPos() end
                c:SetAttribute("SavingOriginalPos", true)
                getgenv().originalFallenHeight = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
                Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0
                local originalPos = XOCU_getOriginalPos()
                if originalPos then cameraAnchor:attach(originalPos) end
                hrp:PivotTo(CFrame.new(tRoot.Position + XOCU_TELEPORT_OFFSET))
                XOCU_setNoCollideChar(tChar)
                replicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(tRoot, tRoot.CFrame)
                task.wait(0.05)
                replicatedStorage.GrabEvents.DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(tRoot)
                task.wait(0.05)
                if tHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and tHead.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    XOCU_modifyTarget(tRoot, tHum)
                end
                XOCU_scheduleReturnHome()
            end)
        else
            if LoopKillHB then LoopKillHB:Disconnect() LoopKillHB = nil end
            cameraAnchor:detach()
        end
    end,
})

-- ---------- Blobman Kill (XOCU ソースそのまま) ----------
local BlobKillEnabled = false
RagdollGroup:AddToggle("KillMode_BlobmanKill", {
    Text = "Blobman Kill",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        BlobKillEnabled = arg
        if arg then
            task.spawn(function()
                while BlobKillEnabled do
                    task.wait(0.01)
                    local v28 = selectedTargetPlayerObj
                    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                    if not (myHum and myHum.SeatPart and myHum.SeatPart.Parent
                        and myHum.SeatPart.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidCreature")) then
                        task.wait(0.3); continue
                    end
                    local parent = myHum.SeatPart.Parent
                    parent.HumanoidCreature:ChangeState(2)
                    local grabScript = parent:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                    local blobHRP    = parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local leftDet    = parent:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                    if not (grabScript and blobHRP and leftDet) then task.wait(0.3); continue end
                    local cGrab    = grabScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")
                    local cRelease = grabScript:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease")
                    local leftWeld = leftDet:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                    if not (cGrab and cRelease and leftWeld) then task.wait(0.3); continue end
                    leftWeld.Enabled = false
                    if v28 and v28.Character then
                        local v32, v33, v34 = GetHRH(v28.Character)
                        if v32 and v33 and v34 and v34.Health > 0 then
                            local saveCF = blobHRP.CFrame
                            local startT = tick()
                            while true do
                                task.wait()
                                Teleport(blobHRP, v33.CFrame, CFrame.new(0, 5, 20))
                                cGrab:FireServer(leftDet, v33, leftWeld)
                                local done = not BlobKillEnabled
                                    or not myHum.SeatPart or myHum.SeatPart.Parent ~= parent
                                    or not v33 or not v34 or v34.Health <= 0
                                    or not leftDet or not leftWeld
                                    or (leftWeld.Attachment0 and leftWeld.Attachment0.Parent == v33)
                                    or tick() - startT > 1.5
                                if not done then continue end
                                break
                            end
                            if myHum.SeatPart and myHum.SeatPart.Parent == parent
                                and leftWeld.Attachment0 and leftWeld.Attachment0.Parent == v33
                                and v34 and v34.Health > 0 then
                                cRelease:FireServer(leftWeld)
                                v34:ChangeState("Dead")
                            else
                                task.wait(1.5)
                            end
                            if blobHRP then Teleport(blobHRP, saveCF, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)) end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

-- ---------- Loop Target Kill (Nil'e ソースそのまま) ----------
local LoopTargetEnabled = false
RagdollGroup:AddToggle("KillMode_NileLoopKill", {
    Text = "Loop Target Kill (Nil'e)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(arg)
        LoopTargetEnabled = arg
        local v27, v28
        task.spawn(function()
    while LoopTargetEnabled do
        local target = selectedTargetPlayerObj
        if not target then task.wait(0.5); continue end
        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        if myChar and myChar.Parent then
            local v30, v31, v32 = GetHRH(myChar)
                    if v30 and v31 and v32 and v32.Health > 0 and target and target.Character
                        and target.InPlot and not target.InPlot.Value then
                        local v33, v34, v35 = GetHRH(target.Character)
                        if v33 and v34 and v35 and v35.Health > 0 then
                            local cFrame = v31.CFrame
                            local fpdh = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
                            local startT = tick()
                            while true do
                                task.wait()
                                Teleport(v31, v33.CFrame, CFrame.new(0, -1.5, 3))
                                v27 = Ownerboat(v33)
                                if not (not LoopTargetEnabled or not v31 or not v33 or not v35 or v35.Health <= 0 or v27 or tick() - startT > 1.5) then
                                    continue
                                end
                                break
                            end
                            if v31 then Teleport(v31, cFrame, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)) end
                            if v27 then
                                Teleport(v34, CFrame.new(0, -50000, 0), CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -45000
                                task.wait()
                                Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = fpdh
                            end
                            task.wait(0.4)
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end)
    end,
})

-- =====================================================
-- Main タブ
-- =====================================================
local MainGroup = Tabs.Main:AddLeftGroupbox("3D View", "user")

local viewportHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
viewportHolder.Name = "Clyrix3DView"
viewportHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 240)
viewportHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
viewportHolder.ClipsDescendants = true
viewportHolder.LayoutOrder = -1

local viewport = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
viewport.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
viewport.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 25)
viewport.BorderSizePixel = 0
viewport.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
viewport.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
viewport.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1)
viewport.Parent = viewportHolder

local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
corner.Parent = viewport

local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 100)
stroke.Thickness = 1
stroke.Parent = viewport

local worldModel = Instance.new("WorldModel")
worldModel.Parent = viewport

local viewCam = Instance.new("Camera")
viewCam.FieldOfView = 40
viewCam.Parent = viewport
viewport.CurrentCamera = viewCam

local inputBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
inputBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
inputBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
inputBtn.Text = ""
inputBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
inputBtn.Parent = viewportHolder

local rotX, rotY, zoomMul = 0, 0, 1.0
local dragging = false
local lastPos = Vector2.new()

local function buildClone()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    local wasArc = char.Archivable
    pcall(function() char.Archivable = true end)
    for _, v in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        pcall(function() v.Archivable = true end)
    end
    local ok, clone = pcall(function() return char:Clone() end)
    pcall(function() char.Archivable = wasArc end)
    if not ok or not clone then return nil end
    for _, v in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        pcall(function()
            if v:IsA("BasePart") then
                v.Anchored = true
                v.CanCollide = false
                v.CanTouch = false
                v.CanQuery = false
                v.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
            elseif v:IsA("Script") or v:IsA("LocalScript") or v:IsA("Sound")
                or v:IsA("BodyMover") or v:IsA("AlignPosition") or v:IsA("AlignOrientation")
                or v:IsA("BodyPosition") or v:IsA("BodyGyro") or v:IsA("BodyVelocity")
                or v:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity") or v:IsA("BodyForce") or v:IsA("BodyThrust")
                or v:IsA("RocketPropulsion") or v:IsA("VectorForce") or v:IsA("LinearVelocity")
                or v:IsA("AngularVelocity") then
                v:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
    return clone
end

local function render()
    pcall(function() worldModel:ClearAllChildren() end)
    local clone = buildClone()
    if not clone then return end
    clone.Parent = worldModel
    local ref = clone:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or clone:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or clone:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        or clone:FindFirstChild("Head")
        or clone:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if not ref then return end
    local origin = ref.Position
    local look = ref.CFrame.LookVector
    local theta = math.atan2(look.X, look.Z)
    local rotOffset = CFrame.Angles(0, -theta, 0)
    local minV, maxV
    for _, v in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function()
                v.CFrame = rotOffset * (v.CFrame - origin)
                if v.Transparency < 1 then
                    local p = v.Position
                    local h = v.Size * 0.5
                    local mins = p - h
                    local maxs = p + h
                    if not minV then minV, maxV = mins, maxs
                    else
                        minV = Vector3.new(math.min(minV.X, mins.X), math.min(minV.Y, mins.Y), math.min(minV.Z, mins.Z))
                        maxV = Vector3.new(math.max(maxV.X, maxs.X), math.max(maxV.Y, maxs.Y), math.max(maxV.Z, maxs.Z))
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    if not minV then minV = Vector3.new(-2, -3, -1); maxV = Vector3.new(2, 3, 1) end
    local center = (minV + maxV) * 0.5
    local sizeVec = maxV - minV
    local charHeight = math.max(sizeVec.Y, 3)
    for _, v in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function() v.CFrame = v.CFrame - center end)
        end
    end
    local baseDist = (charHeight * 0.5) / math.tan(math.rad(viewCam.FieldOfView * 0.5))
    baseDist = baseDist * 1.3
    local dist = baseDist * zoomMul
    local pitch = math.rad(rotX)
    local yaw = math.rad(rotY)
    local rotCF = CFrame.Angles(pitch, yaw, 0)
    local lookAt = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    local camPos = rotCF * CFrame.new(0, 0, dist)
    viewCam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camPos.Position, lookAt)
end

local placed = false
do
    local lbl = MainGroup:AddLabel("")
    local labelFrame = nil
    if typeof(lbl) == "Instance" and lbl:IsA("GuiObject") then labelFrame = lbl
    elseif type(lbl) == "table" then
        for _, v in pairs(lbl) do
            if typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("GuiObject") then labelFrame = v; break end
        end
    end
    if labelFrame and labelFrame.Parent then
        viewportHolder.Parent = labelFrame.Parent
        viewportHolder.LayoutOrder = -1
        pcall(function()
            labelFrame.Visible = false
            labelFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        end)
        placed = true
    end
end

if not placed then
    task.spawn(function()
        local function getRoot() return (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") end
        local function findTitle(parent, depth)
            if depth > 10 then return nil end
            for _, c in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("TextLabel") and c.Text == "3D View" then return c end
                local f = findTitle(c, depth + 1)
                if f then return f end
            end
            return nil
        end
        for _ = 1, 100 do
            local t = findTitle(getRoot(), 0)
            if t then
                local gb = t.Parent
                for _, sib in ipairs(gb:GetChildren()) do
                    if (sib:IsA("Frame") or sib:IsA("ScrollingFrame"))
                        and (sib:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout") or sib:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGridLayout")) then
                        viewportHolder.Parent = sib
                        viewportHolder.LayoutOrder = -1
                        return
                    end
                end
                viewportHolder.Parent = gb
                viewportHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 32)
                viewportHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 240)
                return
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end

inputBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        lastPos = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
    end
end)
inputBtn.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if not dragging then return end
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        local cur = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        local delta = cur - lastPos
        rotY = rotY - delta.X * 0.6
        rotX = math.clamp(rotX + delta.Y * 0.6, -80, 80)
        lastPos = cur
        render()
    end
end)
inputBtn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = false
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.5)
    render()
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(1)
        render()
    end)
    while task.wait(2) do
        if not dragging then render() end
    end
end)

CameraGroup:AddSlider("CamFOV", { Text = "Field of View", Default = 70, Min = 1, Max = 120, Rounding = 1, Suffix = "°",
    Callback = function(Value) Camera.FieldOfView = Value end })
CameraGroup:AddToggle("CamThirdPerson", { Text = "Third Person", Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        if Value then
            LocalPlayer.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.Classic
            LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 1000
            LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
        else
            LocalPlayer.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson
            LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 0.5
            LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
        end
    end })

-- Player Info
local InfoGroup = Tabs.Main:AddRightGroupbox("Player Information", "info")
local DisplayNameLabel = InfoGroup:AddLabel("表示名: -")
local UserIdLabel     = InfoGroup:AddLabel("プレイヤーID: -")
local PlayTimeLabel   = InfoGroup:AddLabel("プレイ時間: -")
local PositionLabel   = InfoGroup:AddLabel("座標: -")
local BlockLabel      = InfoGroup:AddLabel("足の下のブロック: -")

local function setLabel(label, text)
    if not label then return end
    if typeof(label) == "table" and label.SetText then
        pcall(function() label:SetText(text) end)
    elseif typeof(label) == "Instance" and label:IsA("TextLabel") then
        label.Text = text
    end
end

local startTime = tick()
local function formatTime(seconds)
    seconds = math.floor(seconds)
    local h = math.floor(seconds / 3600)
    local m = math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60)
    local s = seconds % 60
    return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", h, m, s)
end
local function getBlockUnderFeet()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return "-" end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return "-" end
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
    local result = Workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(0, -1, 0) * 500, params)
    if result and result.Instance then return result.Instance.Name end
    return "-"
end
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.2) do
        pcall(function()
            setLabel(DisplayNameLabel, "表示名: " .. tostring(LocalPlayer.DisplayName))
            setLabel(UserIdLabel, "プレイヤーID: " .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId))
            setLabel(PlayTimeLabel, "プレイ時間: " .. formatTime(tick() - startTime))
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                local p = hrp.Position
                setLabel(PositionLabel, string.format("座標: X=%.1f, Y=%.1f, Z=%.1f", p.X, p.Y, p.Z))
            else
                setLabel(PositionLabel, "座標: -")
            end
            setLabel(BlockLabel, "足の下のブロック: " .. getBlockUnderFeet())
        end)
    end
end)

-- =====================================================
-- Player タブ: Mode
-- =====================================================
local ModeGroup = Tabs.Player:AddLeftGroupbox("Mode", "settings")
local function getChar2() return LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait() end

local walkCfg = { enabled = false, speed = 1 }
ModeGroup:AddToggle("WalkSpeedToggle", { Text = "Walk Speed", Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value) walkCfg.enabled = Value end })
ModeGroup:AddSlider("WalkSpeedSlider", { Text = "Walk Speed Value", Default = 1, Min = 0.1, Max = 10, Rounding = 1,
    Callback = function(Value) walkCfg.speed = Value end })

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait()
        if walkCfg.enabled then
            pcall(function()
                local char = getChar2()
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hrp and hum and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                    local md = hum.MoveDirection
                    hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(md.X, 0, md.Z).Unit * walkCfg.speed
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

local jumpCfg = { enabled = false, power = 50 }
ModeGroup:AddToggle("JumpPowerToggle", { Text = "Jump Power", Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        jumpCfg.enabled = Value
        if not Value then
            pcall(function()
                local hum = getChar2():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then hum.JumpPower = 50 end
            end)
        end
    end })
ModeGroup:AddSlider("JumpPowerSlider", { Text = "Jump Power Value", Default = 50, Min = 0, Max = 500, Rounding = 0,
    Callback = function(Value)
        jumpCfg.power = Value
        if jumpCfg.enabled then
            pcall(function()
                local hum = getChar2():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then hum.JumpPower = Value end
            end)
        end
    end })

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)
        if jumpCfg.enabled then
            pcall(function()
                local hum = getChar2():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.JumpPower ~= jumpCfg.power then hum.JumpPower = jumpCfg.power end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

local infJumpEnabled = false
ModeGroup:AddToggle("InfiniteJumpToggle", { Text = "Infinite Jump", Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value) infJumpEnabled = Value end })
UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if not infJumpEnabled then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end
    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
end)

        local connection3 = nil
        setupHiddenMove = function()
            cframe = arg3.CFrame; cframe2 = arg4.CFrame; cframe3 = arg3.CFrame

                if arg3.Parent then arg3.CFrame = cframe end
                if arg4.Parent then arg4.CFrame = cframe2 end

                if not arg3.Parent or not arg4.Parent then return end
                cframe = arg3.CFrame; cframe2 = arg4.CFrame
                arg3.CFrame = cframe3; arg4.CFrame = cframe3
            end)
        end
        cleanupHiddenMove = function()
            pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RSCClient") end)
            if connection3 then connection3:Disconnect(); connection3 = nil end
            if arg4.Parent then arg4.CFrame = cframe2 end
            if arg3.Parent then arg3.CFrame = cframe end
        end

            if arg6 then setupHiddenMove() end
            while arg.Parent and not tbl17.ispartowner(arg) and arg5.Health ~= 0 and arg3.Parent and tick() - now < n8 do
                if arg6 then cframe3 = arg.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, 22, 0) end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                tbl17.grab(arg, arg6 and cframe3 or nil)
            end
        end)
        if arg6 then pcall(cleanupHiddenMove) end
        return ok2, result2
    end
    tbl17.sno = function(arg, arg2)
        if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" or not arg:IsA("BasePart") or not arg.Parent then return false end

        end

        tbl20.States.Owning = false
        if typeof(v25) == "Instance" then task.delay(1, tbl17.dt, v25) end
        if not v26 then return false end
        return tbl17.ispartowner(arg)
    end

    tbl17.teleport = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
        if not arg then return false end
        if typeof(arg) == "Instance" and arg:IsA("Model") then
            arg = arg.PrimaryPart or arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        end
        if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" or not arg:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
        local cframe
        if typeof(arg2) == "CFrame" then cframe = arg2
        elseif typeof(arg2) == "Vector3" then cframe = CFrame.new(arg2)
        else
            cframe = nil
            if typeof(arg2) == "Instance" then
                if arg2:IsA("BasePart") then cframe = arg2.CFrame
                else
                    cframe = nil
                    if arg2:IsA("Model") then cframe = arg2:GetPivot() end
                end
            end
        end
        if not cframe then return false end
        if typeof(arg3) == "CFrame" then cframe *= arg3 end
        arg.CFrame = cframe
        tbl17.resetVelocity(arg)
        return true
    end
    tbl17.resetVelocity = function(arg)

            end
        end
    end

                tbl17.teleport(v23, CFrame.new(0, 9e9, 0))
                v23.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 9e9, 0)
                task.delay(0.35, tbl17.unsno, v23)

        end
    end
    tbl17.grab = function(arg, arg2)
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()

        if typeof(arg2) ~= "CFrame" then return end

    end
    limbMatchesMode = function(arg, arg2)
        if not arg:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
        local flag2 = arg.Name:find("Arm") ~= nil
        local flag3 = arg.Name:find("Leg") ~= nil
        if arg2 == "Arms" then return flag2 end
        if arg2 == "Legs" then return flag3 end
        if arg2 == "All" then return flag2 or flag3 end

    end
    tbl17.breaklimb = function(arg, arg2)
        local flag2 = arg == localPlayer
        if flag2 and tbl19.GucciAntiGrab then return false end
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp(arg)
        local ragdolled = v24 and v24:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
        if not v23 or not v24 or v24.Health == 0 or not ragdolled then return false end
        local children = v22:GetChildren()
        local flag3 = false
        for _, child in ipairs(children) do
            if limbMatchesMode(child, arg2) then flag3 = true; break end
        end
        if not flag3 then return false end
        if flag2 then
            refreshRemotes()
            if not tbl23.RagdollRemote then return false end
            fireRemote(tbl23.RagdollRemote, v23, 0.35)
            local now = tick()
            while true do
                task.wait()
                if ragdolled.Value then break
                elseif tick() - now > 0.5 then break end
            end
        else
            if not tbl17.sno(v23) then return false end
            if not tbl17.ragdoll(v22) then return false end
        end
        if not ragdolled.Value then return false end
        for _, child in ipairs(children) do
            if limbMatchesMode(child, arg2) then child.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -100000, 0) end
        end
        task.wait(0.15)
        if flag2 then
            tbl17.grab(v23)
            task.wait()
            refreshRemotes()
            if tbl23.RagdollRemote then fireRemote(tbl23.RagdollRemote, v23, 0) end
        else
            Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -1000
            tbl17.teleport(v23, CFrame.new(0, -1100, 0))
            task.wait()
            Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = tbl19.DestroyVoid and (0/0) or -100
            tbl17.unsno(v23)
        end
        return true
    end
    recoveredYield = function(arg)
        local n8 = arg or 0
        local now = tick()

        return true
    end
    tbl17.ExtraEscape = function()
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
        if not v23 or not v24 or v24.Health == 0 or tbl20.States.Escaping then return end
        local inPlot = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
        if not inPlot or inPlot.Value then return end
        tbl20.States.Escaping = true
        local waypoints = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Waypoints")
        waypoints = waypoints and waypoints:FindFirstChild("TudorHouse")
        if not waypoints then tbl20.States.Escaping = false; return end
        local TractorGreen = tbl17.st("TractorGreen", waypoints.CFrame)
        if not TractorGreen then tbl20.States.Escaping = false; return end
        local vehicleSeat = TractorGreen:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 1)
        if not vehicleSeat then tbl20.States.Escaping = false; return tbl17.dt(TractorGreen) end
        local now = tick(); local flag2 = false
        v24.Sit = false
        vehicleSeat:Sit(v24)
        local connection3 = vehicleSeat.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "SeatWeld" then
                child.Enabled = false; flag2 = true; child:Destroy()
            end
        end)
        while true do
            recoveredYield()
            if flag2 then break
            elseif tick() - now > 0.7 then break end
        end
        connection3:Disconnect()
        v24.Sit = false
        tbl20.States.Escaping = false
        return tbl17.dt(TractorGreen)
    end
    breakVehicleSeat = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
        if tbl19.GucciAntiGrab then return false end
        if not arg or not arg.Parent then return false end
        if not (arg:IsA("VehicleSeat") or arg:IsA("Seat")) then return false end
        if arg.Occupant or arg:GetAttribute("broken") then return false end
        if not arg2 or arg2.Health == 0 or not arg3 then return false end
        arg2.Sit = false
        if tbl19.RagdollInRG and arg4 then tbl17.sno(arg4, 1.5) end
        arg.CanCollide = false
        local flag2 = false
        local connection3 = arg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "SeatWeld" then
                pcall(function() child.Enabled = false end)
                child:Destroy()
                flag2 = true
            end
        end)
        local now = tick()
        while arg.Parent and arg2.Parent and not flag2 and tick() - now <= 0.5 do
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            arg:Sit(arg2)
        end
        arg2.Sit = false
        local parent = arg.Parent
        task.delay(0.5, function()
            if arg and parent then
                pcall(function()
                    arg.Parent = replicatedStorage
                    task.wait()
                    arg.Parent = parent
                end)
            end
        end)
        if flag2 then
            arg:SetAttribute("broken", true)
            if connection3 then connection3:Disconnect() end
            local connection4 = nil
            connection4 = arg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name ~= "SeatWeld" then return end
                local v22 = tbl17.charp()
                local part1 = nil
                pcall(function() part1 = child.Part1 end)
                if part1 ~= v22 then
                    arg:SetAttribute("broken", false)
                    if connection4 then connection4:Disconnect() end
                end
            end)
            return true
        end
        arg2.Sit = true
        task.wait(0.05)
        arg2.Sit = false
        if connection3 then connection3:Disconnect() end
        return false
    end
    applySeatBreakerToInventory = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
        if not arg then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Model") then
                local vehicleSeat = child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat") or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Seat")
                if vehicleSeat then breakVehicleSeat(vehicleSeat, arg2, arg3, arg4) end
            end
        end
    end
    tbl17.RemoveGucci = function(arg)
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()

        local v28, v29 = tbl17.getInventory(arg)
        if not v24 or v24.Health <= 0 or not v23 or not v28 or not v27 then return false end
        local seatPart = v27.SeatPart
        if not seatPart then
            applySeatBreakerToInventory(v28, v24, v23, v26)
            if v29 then applySeatBreakerToInventory(v29, v24, v23, v26) end
            return nil
        end
        if v27.Sit then
            applySeatBreakerToInventory(v28, v24, v23, v26)
            if v29 then applySeatBreakerToInventory(v29, v24, v23, v26) end
            return nil
        end
        seatPart:SetAttribute("broken", false)
        return breakVehicleSeat(seatPart, v24, v23, v26)
    end
    tbl17.DeleteSeatables = function(arg)
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
        local v25, v26 = tbl17.getInventory(arg)
        if not v24 or v24.Health <= 0 or not v23 or not v25 then return false end
        processFolder = function(arg2)
            if not arg2 then return end
            for _, child in ipairs(arg2:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Model") then
                    local primaryPart = child.PrimaryPart or tbl17.findNicePart(child)
                    if primaryPart and primaryPart:IsA("BasePart") and primaryPart.Velocity.Magnitude <= 10 then
                        local vehicleSeat = child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
                        if vehicleSeat and not vehicleSeat.Occupant then tbl17.DestroyObject(child) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        processFolder(v25)
        if v26 then processFolder(v26) end
        return nil
    end
    tbl17.EatAntiInput = function(arg)
        local character = arg and arg.Character
        character = character and character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
        local leftArm = v22 and v22:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
        leftArm = leftArm and leftArm:FindFirstChild("LeftGripAttachment")
        local plotItems = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
        if not character or not v22 or not leftArm or not v24 or v24.Health == 0 or not plotItems then return false end
        local flag2 = false
        for _, v25 in pairs(character:GetConnectedParts()) do
            if v25.Name == "HoldPart" and not v25:IsDescendantOf(plotItems) then
                local parent = v25.Parent
                local holdItemRemoteFunction = v25:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 0.45)
                local dropItemRemoteFunction = v25:WaitForChild("DropItemRemoteFunction", 0.45)
                local rigidConstraint = v25:WaitForChild("RigidConstraint", 0.45)
                if holdItemRemoteFunction and dropItemRemoteFunction and rigidConstraint then
                    rigidConstraint.Enabled = false
                    local now = tick()
                    while v22.Parent and parent.Parent and leftArm.Parent and rigidConstraint.Parent and rigidConstraint.Attachment1 ~= leftArm and v24.Health ~= 0 and tick() - now < 1 do
                        task.spawn(function()
                            pcall(function() holdItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(parent, v22) end)
                        end)
                        task.wait()
                    end
                    if rigidConstraint.Parent and rigidConstraint.Attachment1 == leftArm then
                        task.spawn(function()
                            pcall(function() dropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(parent) end)
                        end)
                        pcall(function() dropItemRemoteFunction:Destroy() end)
                        flag2 = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return flag2
    end

    tbl17.ToggleCollisions = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
        if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" or not arg:IsA("Model") then return end

                else

                    if not arg3 then
                        local ragdollLimbPart = child:FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart")
                        if ragdollLimbPart then ragdollLimbPart.CanCollide = false end
                    end
                end
                if child.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    local physicalProperties = nil
                    if arg4 then physicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(0.01, 0.01, 0.01, 0.01, 0.01) end
                    child.CustomPhysicalProperties = physicalProperties
                end
            end
        end
    end

            if v23 and v23:IsA("BasePart") then
                v23.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame * v22
