            end
        end
        return true
    end
    tbl17.removesticky = function(arg)
        if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" or not arg:IsA("Model") or not arg.Parent then return false end
        local humanoidRootPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoidRootPart:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
        local plotItems = Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
        local obj3 = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        registerModel = function(arg2)
            if typeof(arg2) ~= "Instance" then return end
            local parent
            if arg2:IsA("BasePart") then parent = arg2.Parent
            else parent = arg2 end
            if parent and parent:IsA("Model") and parent ~= arg and parent.Parent then obj3[parent] = true end
        end
        for _, descendant in pairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                for _, v22 in pairs(descendant:GetConnectedParts()) do
                    if v22.Name == "StickyPart" then
                        for _, child in pairs(v22:GetChildren()) do
                            if child:IsA("TouchInterest") then child:Destroy() end
                        end
                        registerModel(v22)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local playerFromCharacter = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(arg)
        if playerFromCharacter then
            local v22, v23 = tbl17.getInventory(playerFromCharacter)
            if v22 then for _, child in pairs(v22:GetChildren()) do registerModel(child) end end
            if v23 then for _, child in pairs(v23:GetChildren()) do registerModel(child) end end
        end
        local flag2 = false
        for k in pairs(obj3) do
            if k.Parent then
                local ok2, result2 = pcall(k.GetPivot, k)
                if ok2 and (result2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
                    local v22 = tbl17.findNicePart(k)
                    if v22 and v22:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local rscBV = v22:FindFirstChild("RscBV")
                        if rscBV and not rscBV:IsA("BodyVelocity") then rscBV:Destroy(); rscBV = nil end
                        if not rscBV then
                            local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                            bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -50000, 0)
                            bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                            bodyVelocity.Name = "RscBV"
                            bodyVelocity.Parent = v22
                        end
                        tbl17.ToggleCollisions(k, false)
                        if not (plotItems and k:IsDescendantOf(plotItems)) then tbl17.grab(v22) end
                        local stickyPart = k:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                        if stickyPart then for _, child in pairs(stickyPart:GetChildren()) do child:Destroy() end end
                        k:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, -50000, 0))
                        task.delay(1, tbl17.unsno, v22)
                        flag2 = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return flag2
    end
    tbl17.ragdoll = function(arg, arg2)
        if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" then return end
        local torso_, humanoid_
        if arg:IsA("Player") then
            local character = arg.Character
            torso_ = nil; humanoid_ = nil
            if character then
                torso_ = character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                humanoid_ = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            end
        elseif arg:IsA("Model") then
            local head_ = arg:FindFirstChild("Head")
            torso_ = arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            humanoid_ = arg:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if not head_ then return end
        else
            if not arg:IsA("BasePart") then return end
            local isModel = arg.Parent and arg.Parent:IsA("Model")
            humanoid_ = nil
            if isModel then
                humanoid_ = arg.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                torso_ = arg
            else torso_ = arg end
        end
        if not torso_ or not torso_:IsA("BasePart") or not torso_.Parent or not humanoid_ then return end
        local ragdolled = humanoid_:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
        if not ragdolled then return end
        if ragdolled.Value ~= false then return end
        if tbl20.States.Ragdolling then return end
        torso_.CanCollide = false
        tbl20.States.Ragdolling = true
        local v22 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
        if not v22 then tbl20.States.Ragdolling = false; return end
        local ragdollRSC = v22:FindFirstChild("RagdollRSC") or tbl17.st("PalletLightBrown")
        if not ragdollRSC then tbl20.States.Ragdolling = false; return end
        local soundPart = ragdollRSC:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 0.5)
        if not soundPart or not soundPart:IsA("BasePart") then
            tbl20.States.Ragdolling = false; tbl17.dt(ragdollRSC); return
        end
        local main = ragdollRSC:WaitForChild("Main", 0.5)
        if not main or not main:IsA("BasePart") then
            tbl20.States.Ragdolling = false; tbl17.dt(ragdollRSC); return
        end
        if not tbl17.sno(soundPart) then
            tbl20.States.Ragdolling = false; tbl17.dt(ragdollRSC); return
        end
        ragdollRSC.Name = "RagdollRSC"
        local ragdollBP = main:FindFirstChild("RagdollBP")
        if ragdollBP and not ragdollBP:IsA("BodyPosition") then ragdollBP:Destroy(); ragdollBP = nil end
        if not ragdollBP then
            local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
            bodyPosition.Name = "RagdollBP"; bodyPosition.D = 1500
            bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
            bodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(0, 100000, 0)
            bodyPosition.Parent = main
        else
            ragdollBP.D = 1500
            ragdollBP.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
            ragdollBP.Position = Vector3.new(0, 100000, 0)
        end
        local now = tick()
        while ragdollRSC.Parent and soundPart.Parent and torso_.Parent and ragdolled.Parent and ragdolled.Value ~= true and tick() - now <= 0.5 do
            soundPart.Position = torso_.Position
            soundPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -10, 0)
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        tbl20.States.Ragdolling = false
        return ragdolled.Value
    end
    tbl17.st = function(arg, arg2)
        if type(arg) ~= "string" or tbl20.FailedToys[arg] then return nil end
        local v22, v23 = tbl17.charp()
        if not v23 or tbl20.States.Spawning then return nil end
        local inPlot = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
        local inOwnedPlot = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot")
        local flag2 = inPlot ~= nil and inPlot.Value == true
        if flag2 then flag2 = not (inOwnedPlot ~= nil and inOwnedPlot.Value == true) end
        if flag2 and tbl20.States.Owning then return nil end
        refreshRemotes()
        if not tbl23.spawnEvent or not tbl23.spawnEvent:IsA("RemoteFunction") then return nil end
        local v24 = Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
        if not v24 then return nil end
        tbl20.States.Spawning = true
        if flag2 then tbl20.States.Owning = true end
        local connection3 = nil; local connection4 = nil; local flag3 = false
        local cFrame = v23.CFrame; local v25 = nil
        cleanup = function()
            if flag3 then
                pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("STClient") end)
                flag3 = false
            end
            if connection4 then connection4:Disconnect(); connection4 = nil end
            if connection3 then connection3:Disconnect(); connection3 = nil end
            if flag2 and v23 and v23.Parent then v23.CFrame = cFrame end
            tbl20.States.Spawning = false
            if flag2 then tbl20.States.Owning = false end
        end
        local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
            local canSpawnToy = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
            if canSpawnToy then
                while canSpawnToy.Value == false do
                    task.wait()
                    if not v23.Parent then return end
                end
            end
            local cframe = flag2 and CFrame.new(0, 25, 0) or v23.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 15, 0)
            local n8 = CFrame.new((typeof(arg2) == "CFrame" and arg2 or cframe).Position) + Vector3.new(0, 0, 20)
            local rotateOrientPart = Workspace:FindFirstChild("RotateOrientPart")
            local rotation = rotateOrientPart and rotateOrientPart.Rotation or Vector3.zero
            connection3 = v24.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name == arg then
                    v25 = child
                    if connection3 then connection3:Disconnect(); connection3 = nil end
                end
            end)
            cFrame = v23.CFrame
            if flag2 then
                local cframe2 = CFrame.new(0, 10, 0)
                pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("STClient") end)
                RunService:BindToRenderStep("STClient", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                    if v23.Parent then v23.CFrame = cFrame end
                end)
                flag3 = true
                connection4 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if v23.Parent then
                        cFrame = v23.CFrame
                        v23.CFrame = cframe2
                    end
                end)
            end
            invokeSpawn = function() fireRemote(tbl23.spawnEvent, arg, n8, rotation) end
            task.spawn(invokeSpawn)
            local now = tick()
            while true do
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                if flag2 then task.spawn(invokeSpawn) end
                local v26 = v25; local flag4
                if v25 then flag4 = v26
                else flag4 = tick() - now > 1.3 end
                if not flag4 then continue end
                break
            end
        end)
        cleanup()
        if not ok2 then return nil end
        if not v25 then
            tbl20.FailedToys[arg] = true
            task.delay(2, function() tbl20.FailedToys[arg] = nil end)
        end
        return v25
    end
    tbl17.dt = function(arg)
        if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" or not arg:IsA("Model") then return false end
        refreshRemotes()
        if not tbl23.destroyToyEvent then return false end
        return fireRemote(tbl23.destroyToyEvent, arg) == true
    end
    removeTargetAntiKickShuriken = function(arg)
        if not arg then return end
        destroyStickyOwner = function(arg2)
            if not arg2 or not arg2:IsA("BasePart") then return end
            for _, v22 in ipairs(arg2:GetConnectedParts()) do
                if v22.Name == "StickyPart" then
                    local parent = v22.Parent
                    if parent and (parent.Name == "NinjaShuriken" or parent.Name == "ShurikenRSC") then
                        tbl17.dt(parent)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then destroyStickyOwner(descendant) end
        end
    end
    cleanupNetworkKickPlayer = function(arg)
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp(arg)
        if not v22 or not v23 or not v24 then return false end
        local kickAP = v23:FindFirstChild("KickAP")
        local kickAO = v23:FindFirstChild("KickAO")
        local weldHRP = v23:FindFirstChild("WeldHRP")
        if kickAP then kickAP:Destroy() end
        if kickAO then kickAO:Destroy() end
        local ragdolled = v24:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
        if ragdolled and ragdolled.Value == true and weldHRP then weldHRP.Enabled = true end
        task.spawn(tbl17.ToggleMotor6D, v22, true, true)
        task.spawn(tbl17.ToggleCollisions, v22, true)
        return true
    end
    createNetworkKickMovers = function(parent, position)
        local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
        bodyPosition.Name = "KickAP"; bodyPosition.D = 500
        bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bodyPosition.Position = position
        bodyPosition.Parent = parent
        local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        bodyGyro.Name = "KickAO"; bodyGyro.D = 500
        bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.new()
        bodyGyro.Parent = parent
        return bodyPosition, bodyGyro
    end
    prepareNetworkKickMovers = function(arg, position)
        local kickAP = arg:FindFirstChild("KickAP")
        local kickAO = arg:FindFirstChild("KickAO")
        local weldHRP = arg:FindFirstChild("WeldHRP")
        if not kickAP or not kickAO then
            if kickAP then kickAP:Destroy() end
            if kickAO then kickAO:Destroy() end
            kickAP, kickAO = createNetworkKickMovers(arg, position)
        end
        if weldHRP then weldHRP.Enabled = false end
        kickAP.Position = position
        return kickAP, kickAO, weldHRP
    end
    getRecoveredKickPosition = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
        local n8 = (arg2 - 1) * 6.2831853071795862 / arg3
        local nkOffset = tbl18.NKOffset
        return arg.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(n8) * 10, nkOffset, math.sin(n8) * 10)
    end
    performNetworkKickPass = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
        if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" or not arg:IsA("Player") or arg == localPlayer or not arg.Parent then return false end
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
        if not v22 or not v23 or not v24 or v24.Health <= 0 then return false end
        local inPlot = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
        if inPlot and inPlot.Value == true then return false end
        local v25, v26, v27 = tbl17.charp(arg)
        if not v25 or not v26 or not v27 or v27.Health <= 0 then return false end
        if not v25:FindFirstChild("Torso") then return false end
        local v28 = getRecoveredKickPosition(v23, arg2, arg3)
        prepareNetworkKickMovers(v26, v28)
        if tbl17.getMagnitude(v23, v26) > 33 then
            if not tbl17.sno(v26) then return false end
            tbl17.teleport(v26, v28)
        end
        tbl17.grab(v26)
        releaseKickOwnership(v26, tbl18.NKDelayMode)
        if tbl19.RagdollInNK then task.spawn(tbl17.ragdoll, v25) end
        if tbl19.DeleteShurInNK then task.spawn(tbl17.removesticky, v25) end
        tbl17.ToggleCollisions(v25, false)
        tbl17.RepositionLimbs(v25, true)
        if not arg4 then
            task.delay(0.5, function()
                pcall(cleanupNetworkKickPlayer, arg)
                obj[arg] = nil
            end)
        end
        return true
    end
    releaseKickOwnership = function(arg, arg2)
        if arg2 == "Faster but worse" then tbl17.unsno(arg); return end
        if arg2 == "Normal" then task.delay(0.2, tbl17.unsno, arg); return end
        if arg2 == "Slower but better" then
            task.spawn(function() task.wait(); tbl17.unsno(arg) end)
        end
    end
    tbl17.ToggleMotor6D = function(arg, enabled, arg2)
        if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" or not arg:IsA("Model") then return false end
        local humanoid_ = arg:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        local torso_ = arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if not humanoid_ or not torso_ then return false end
        local ragdolled = humanoid_:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
        if not ragdolled then return false end
        if ragdolled.Value ~= true then
            for _, child in pairs(torso_:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Motor6D") then child.Enabled = enabled end
            end
            return true
        end
        if not arg2 then return false end
        for _, v22 in pairs({ "Left Leg", "Right Leg", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Head" }) do
            local v23 = arg:FindFirstChild(v22)
            if v23 then
                local ballSocketConstraint = v23:FindFirstChild("BallSocketConstraint")
                if ballSocketConstraint then ballSocketConstraint.Enabled = enabled end
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    tbl17.NetworkKick = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
        if typeof(arg) ~= "Instance" or not arg:IsA("Player") or arg == localPlayer then return false end
        tbl20.NetworkKickQueue[arg] = { loopCall = arg2 == true, isServer = arg3 == true, queuedAt = tick() }
        return true
    end
    runNetworkKickWorker = function()
        while task.wait() do
            local tbl27 = {}
            for k, v22 in pairs(tbl20.NetworkKickQueue) do
                tbl20.NetworkKickQueue[k] = nil
                if k.Parent and not tbl20.Whitelisted[k] and not obj[k] then
                    table.insert(tbl27, { player = k, request = v22 })
                end
            end
            local n8 = #tbl27
            for i, v22 in ipairs(tbl27) do
                local player = v22.player
                if player.Parent and not obj[player] then
                    obj[player] = true
                    local ok2, result2 = pcall(performNetworkKickPass, player, i, n8, false)
                    local flag2 = not ok2
                    if flag2 or result2 ~= true then obj[player] = nil end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    tbl17.queue = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
        if arg == nil or type(arg2) ~= "function" or not arg3 then return false end
        local tbl27 = tbl25[arg]
        if not tbl27 then tbl27 = {}; tbl25[arg] = tbl27 end
        if not table.find(tbl27, arg3) then table.insert(tbl27, arg3) end
        if tbl26[arg] then return true end
        tbl26[arg] = true
        task.spawn(function()
            while #tbl27 > 0 do
                local v22 = table.remove(tbl27, 1)
                if v22 and v22.Parent then pcall(arg2, v22); task.wait() end
            end
            tbl26[arg] = nil
        end)
        return true
    end
    tbl17.clearAllQueues = function(arg)
        if arg then tbl22[arg] = (tbl22[arg] or 0) + 1
        else for k in pairs(tbl22) do tbl22[k] = tbl22[k] + 1 end end
        for _, v22 in pairs(tbl25) do table.clear(v22) end
        table.clear(tbl20.NetworkKickQueue)
    end
    tbl17.GetTargets = function(arg, arg2)
        local tbl27 = {}
        if arg then
            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= localPlayer and not tbl20.Whitelisted[player] then table.insert(tbl27, player) end
            end
            return tbl27
        end
        local v22 = pairs
        local tbl28 = arg2 or {}
        for k, v23 in v22(tbl28) do
            local flag2 = v23 ~= false
            if not (typeof(k) == "Instance" and k:IsA("Player")) then
                if type(k) == "string" then k = Players:FindFirstChild(k)
                elseif typeof(v23) == "Instance" and v23:IsA("Player") then flag2 = true; k = v23
                else
                    k = nil
                    if type(v23) == "string" then k = Players:FindFirstChild(v23); flag2 = true end
                end
            end
            if k and flag2 and k ~= localPlayer and not tbl20.Whitelisted[k] then table.insert(tbl27, k) end
        end
        return tbl27
    end
    tbl17.ProcessPlayer = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
        if arg4 then return end
        if not arg or arg == localPlayer or tbl20.Whitelisted[arg] then return end
        local targetMethods = tbl20.TargetMethods
        if methodSelected(targetMethods, "Kick") then
            if not arg2 or arg3 then tbl17.NetworkKick(arg, arg2, arg3) end
        end
        if methodSelected(targetMethods, "Kill") then
            if arg2 then tbl17.queue("Kill", tbl17.kill, arg)
            else tbl17.kill(arg) end
        end
        if methodSelected(targetMethods, "Void") then
            if arg2 then tbl17.queue("Void", tbl17.void, arg)
            else tbl17.void(arg) end
        end
        if methodSelected(targetMethods, "Remove Gucci") then
            if arg2 then tbl17.queue("Remove Gucci", tbl17.RemoveGucci, arg)
            else tbl17.RemoveGucci(arg) end
        end
        if methodSelected(targetMethods, "Destroy Food") then
            if arg2 then tbl17.queue("Destroy Food", tbl17.EatAntiInput, arg)
            else tbl17.EatAntiInput(arg) end
        end
        if methodSelected(targetMethods, "Remove Invisibility") then tbl17.RemoveInvisibility(arg) end
        if methodSelected(targetMethods, "Delete Seatables") then
            if arg2 then tbl17.queue("Delete Seatables", tbl17.DeleteSeatables, arg)
            else tbl17.DeleteSeatables(arg) end
        end
        if methodSelected(targetMethods, "Bring") then
            local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp(arg)
            local v25; v25, v25 = tbl17.charp()
            if v23 and v25 and v24 and v24.Health ~= 0 and tbl17.sno(v23) then
                tbl17.teleport(v23, v25, CFrame.new(0, 0, -5))
                task.delay(0.2, tbl17.unsno, v23)
            end
        end
    end
    runSelectedNetworkKickLoop = function()
        while task.wait() do
            local flag2 = tbl19.LoopApplyMethodT == true
            local v22 = methodSelected(tbl20.TargetMethods, "Kick")
            local inPlot = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
            inPlot = inPlot and inPlot.Value == true
            for k in pairs(obj2) do
                if not k or not k.Parent or not flag2 or not v22 or inPlot or tbl20.SelectedPlayers[k] ~= true then
                    pcall(cleanupNetworkKickPlayer, k)
                    obj2[k] = nil
                end
            end
            if flag2 and v22 and not inPlot then
                local v23, v24, v25 = tbl17.charp()
                if v24 and v25 and v25.Health > 0 then
                    local tbl27 = {}
                    for k, selectedPlayer in pairs(tbl20.SelectedPlayers) do
                        if selectedPlayer == true and typeof(k) == "Instance" and k:IsA("Player") and k.Parent then
                            table.insert(tbl27, k)
                            obj2[k] = true
                        end
                    end
                    local n8 = #tbl27
                    if n8 > 0 then
                        for i, v26 in ipairs(tbl27) do
                            if tbl19.LoopApplyMethodT == true then
                                local inPlot2 = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
                                if not (inPlot2 and inPlot2.Value == true) then
                                    local v27, v28, v29 = tbl17.charp()
                                    if not (not v28 or not v29 or v29.Health <= 0) then
                                        pcall(performNetworkKickPass, v26, i, n8, true)
                                        continue
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            break
                        end
                        if tbl18.NKDelayMode == "Slower but better" then task.wait() end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

validateTargetRuntime = function() refreshRemotes(); return true, {} end
tbl17.ValidateTargetRuntime = validateTargetRuntime
task.spawn(runNetworkKickWorker)
task.spawn(runSelectedNetworkKickLoop)

handleCounterAttackChild = function(arg, arg2)
    if not arg:IsA("StringValue") or arg.Name ~= "PartOwner" then return end
    local v22 = Players:FindFirstChild(arg.Value)
    if not v22 then return end
    tbl20.WhoGrabbed = tbl17.FormatPlayer(v22)
    if not tbl19.CounterAttack or tbl20.Whitelisted[v22] then return end
    local v23, v24 = tbl17.charp(v22)
    if tbl18.CounterMethod == "Kill" then tbl17.kill(v22)
    elseif tbl18.CounterMethod == "Void" then tbl17.void(v22)
    elseif tbl18.CounterMethod == "Grab" then tbl17.grab(v24)
    elseif tbl18.CounterMethod == "Delete Limbs" then tbl17.breaklimb(v22, tbl18.TargetedLimbCA)
    elseif tbl18.CounterMethod == "Fling" then
        if v24 and arg2 and tbl17.sno(v24) then
            v24.Velocity = arg2.CFrame.LookVector * 100 + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0)
        end
    elseif tbl18.CounterMethod == "Teleport to Spawn" then
        if v24 and tbl17.sno(v24) then tbl17.teleport(v24, CFrame.new(0, 10, 0)) end
    end
end

bindCounterAttack = function(arg)
    local v22, v23 = tbl17.charp()
    arg = arg and arg:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not arg then return end
    if tbl21.CounterAttack then tbl21.CounterAttack:Disconnect() end
    tbl21.CounterAttack = arg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        handleCounterAttackChild(child, v23)
    end)
end

if localPlayer.Character then task.defer(bindCounterAttack, localPlayer.Character) end
localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
    task.wait()
    bindCounterAttack(character)
end)

-- AntiGrab
do
    local connection3 = nil; local cFrame = nil
    stopAntiGrab = function()
        if connection3 then connection3:Disconnect(); connection3 = nil end
        tbl20.States["Anti Grab"] = false
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
        if v24 then
            if tbl18.AGType == "Anti-Kill" then
                task.delay(0.1, function() if v24.Parent then v24.Sit = false end end)
            end
            v24.AutoRotate = true
        end
    end
    startAntiGrabIfNeeded = function()
        if not tbl19.AntiGrab or not isHeld or not isHeld.Value or tbl20.States["Anti Grab"] then return end
        refreshRemotes()
        if tbl23.Struggle then fireRemote(tbl23.Struggle) end
        local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
        local torso_ = v22 and v22:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        local neck = torso_ and torso_:FindFirstChild("Neck")
        if not v24 or not torso_ then return end
        v24.RequiresNeck = false
        tbl17.resetVelocity(v23 or torso_)
        cFrame = torso_.CFrame
        tbl20.States["Anti Grab"] = true
        local flag2 = tbl18.AGType == "Escapes Anything, but buggy"
        local sit = tbl18.AGType == "Anti-Kill"
        local flag3 = tbl18.AGType == "No Ragdoll"
        if flag2 then task.spawn(tbl17.ExtraEscape) end
        local now = tick()
        connection3 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
            if tbl23.Struggle then fireRemote(tbl23.Struggle) end
            if v23 and not flag2 and not flag3 and tbl23.RagdollRemote then
                fireRemote(tbl23.RagdollRemote, v23, sit and 0.11 or 0)
            end
            if flag2 then
                if tick() - now > 2 then task.spawn(tbl17.ExtraEscape); now = tick() end
            end
            local moveDirection = v24.MoveDirection
            local n8 = 16
            if v24.WalkSpeed > 16 then n8 = v24.WalkSpeed end
            if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                cFrame += moveDirection.Unit * n8 * deltaTime
                local vector = Vector3.new(moveDirection.X, 0, moveDirection.Z)
                if vector.Magnitude > 0 then
                    local position = cFrame.Position
                    cFrame = cFrame:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(position, position + vector.Unit), 0.12)
                end
            end
            tbl17.resetVelocity(v23 or torso_)
            torso_.CFrame = cFrame
            v24.Sit = sit
            task.spawn(tbl17.ToggleCollisions, v22, false)
            if not neck or not neck.Enabled then task.spawn(tbl17.RepositionLimbs, v22) end
            for _, v25 in ipairs(v24:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                local animation = v25.Animation
                if animation and animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://7047322890" then v25:Stop() end
            end
            if v23 then
                local scream = v23:FindFirstChild("Scream")
                if scream then scream.Playing = false end
            end
            if not isHeld or not isHeld.Value or not tbl19.AntiGrab then
                tbl20.States["Anti Grab"] = false
                if sit then task.delay(0.1, function() if v24.Parent then v24.Sit = false end end) end
                v24.AutoRotate = true
                if connection3 then connection3:Disconnect(); connection3 = nil end
            end
        end)
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.03) do
        if tbl19.AntiGrab then startAntiGrabIfNeeded()
        elseif tbl20.States["Anti Grab"] then stopAntiGrab() end
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()

        if tbl19.AntiInputLag then

            local grabEvents2 = v21 and v21:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
            if grabEvents2 and not remoteEvent then
                local endGrabEarly = grabEvents2:FindFirstChild("EndGrabEarly")

            end
            local v23, v24, v25 = tbl17.charp()
            v22 = v22 or tbl17.st(tbl18.AntiInputType, CFrame.new(0, 1e20, 0))
            if v22 and v22.Parent then

                local holdItemRemoteFunction = holdPart and holdPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 2)
                local dropItemRemoteFunction = holdItemRemoteFunction and holdPart:WaitForChild("DropItemRemoteFunction", 2)
                local rigidConstraint = holdPart and holdPart:FindFirstChild("RigidConstraint")
                for _, descendant in ipairs(v22:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                        descendant.CanCollide = false
                        if descendant:IsA("MeshPart") or descendant.Name == "Part" then
                            descendant.Transparency = tbl18.AILTransparency
                            continue
                        end
                        continue
                    end
                    if descendant:IsA("Decal") then descendant.Transparency = tbl18.AILTransparency end
                end
                if v23 and v25 and v25.Health > 0 and holdPart and holdItemRemoteFunction and dropItemRemoteFunction then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        pcall(function()
                            local vector = Vector3.new
                            dropItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(v22, CFrame.new(0, 1e20, 0), vector())
                        end)
                    end)
                    task.spawn(function()
                        pcall(function() holdItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(v22, v23) end)
                    end)
                    if rigidConstraint then
                        local leftArm = v23:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
                        local rightArm = v23:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
                        leftArm = leftArm and leftArm:FindFirstChild("LeftGripAttachment")
                        rightArm = rightArm and rightArm:FindFirstChild("RightGripAttachment")
                        local attachment1 = rigidConstraint.Attachment1
                        if attachment1 and attachment1 ~= leftArm and attachment1 ~= rightArm then
                            tbl17.dt(v22); v22 = nil; continue
                        end
                        continue
                    end
                    continue
                end
                tbl17.dt(v22); v22 = nil
            end
        else
            if v22 then tbl17.dt(v22); v22 = nil end
            if remoteEvent then remoteEvent:Destroy(); remoteEvent = nil end
        end
    end
end)

Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if child.Name ~= "Part" or not tbl19.AntiExplosion or not child:IsA("BasePart") then return end
    local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
    if not v22 or not v23 or not v24 or (child.Position - v23.Position).Magnitude > 20 then return end
    v23.Anchored = true
    task.wait(0.02)
    if not v24.SeatPart then v24.Sit = false end
    local v25 = nil
    pcall(function() v25 = v22:QueryDescendants("#RagdollLimbPart") end)
    if v25 then
        for _, v26 in pairs(v25) do v26.CanCollide = false end
    else
        for _, descendant in ipairs(v22:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and (descendant.Name == "RagdollLimbPart" or descendant:GetAttribute("RagdollLimbPart")) then
                descendant.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end
    tbl17.resetVelocity(v23)
    v23.Anchored = false
end)

bindAntiBurn = function(arg)
    local humanoidRootPart = arg and arg:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 2)
    if not humanoidRootPart then return end
    humanoidRootPart.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name ~= "FireParticleEmitter" or not tbl19.AntiBurn then return end
        local v22 = getMap()
        local hole = v22 and v22:FindFirstChild("Hole")
        hole = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonSmallHole")
        hole = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("ExtinguishPart")
        if not hole or not hole:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        local cFrame = hole.CFrame
        local fireLight = humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("FireLight")
        if fireLight then fireLight:Destroy() end
        if child:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then child.Enabled = false end
        local playerGui2 = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        playerGui2 = playerGui2 and playerGui2:FindFirstChild("ScreenOverlayGui")
        playerGui2 = playerGui2 and playerGui2:FindFirstChild("FireFade")
        if playerGui2 and playerGui2:IsA("GuiObject") then playerGui2.Visible = false end
        while child and child.Parent and humanoidRootPart.Parent do
            hole.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
            task.wait()
        end
        if hole.Parent then hole.CFrame = cFrame end
    end)
end

do
    local v22 = getMap()
    local hole = v22 and v22:FindFirstChild("Hole")
    hole = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("PoisonSmallHole")
    hole = hole and hole:FindFirstChild("ExtinguishPart")
    if hole and hole:IsA("BasePart") then
        local tex = hole:FindFirstChild("Tex")
        if tex then tex:Destroy() end
        hole.Transparency = 1
    end
end

localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(bindAntiBurn)
if localPlayer.Character then task.defer(bindAntiBurn, localPlayer.Character) end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.03) do
        if tbl19.AntiVoid then
            local v22, v23 = tbl17.charp()
            if v23 and (v23.Position.Y >= 10000 or v23.Position.Y <= -500) then

            end
        end
    end
end)

local NinjaShuriken = nil
destroyAntiKickShuriken = function()
    if NinjaShuriken then tbl17.dt(NinjaShuriken); NinjaShuriken = nil end
end
getAntiKickParts = function()
    if not NinjaShuriken or not NinjaShuriken.Parent then return nil, nil end
    local stickyPart = NinjaShuriken:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
    if not stickyPart then return nil, nil end
    return stickyPart, (stickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld"))
end
antiKickAttachedTo = function(arg)
    if not NinjaShuriken or not NinjaShuriken.Parent or NinjaShuriken.Name ~= "ShurikenRSC" then return false end
    local v22, v23 = getAntiKickParts()
    if not v22 or not v23 then return false end
    return v23.Part1 == arg
end
applyAntiKickAppearance = function()
    if not NinjaShuriken or not NinjaShuriken.Parent then return end
    local shurHighlight = NinjaShuriken:FindFirstChild("ShurHighlight")
    if not shurHighlight then
        shurHighlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        shurHighlight.Name = "ShurHighlight"
        shurHighlight.Adornee = NinjaShuriken
        shurHighlight.Parent = NinjaShuriken
    end
    shurHighlight.Enabled = tbl20.AntiKickSettings.Highlight
    shurHighlight.FillColor = tbl20.AntiKickSettings.HighlightFill
    shurHighlight.OutlineColor = tbl20.AntiKickSettings.HighlightOutl
    shurHighlight.FillTransparency = tbl20.AntiKickSettings.HighlightFT
    shurHighlight.OutlineTransparency = tbl20.AntiKickSettings.HighlightOT
    local transparency = tbl20.AntiKickSettings.Transparency
    for _, child in ipairs(NinjaShuriken:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
            if child.Transparency ~= transparency then
                if child:IsA("MeshPart") or child.Name == "Part" then child.Transparency = transparency end
            end
            child.CanCollide = false
            child.CanQuery = false
        end
    end
end
attachAntiKickShuriken = function(arg)
    if not NinjaShuriken or not NinjaShuriken.Parent then
        if NinjaShuriken then tbl17.dt(NinjaShuriken) end
        NinjaShuriken = tbl17.st("NinjaShuriken", arg.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 15, 0))
        if not NinjaShuriken then return false end
    end
    local stickyPart = NinjaShuriken:WaitForChild("StickyPart", 1.5)
    if not stickyPart then destroyAntiKickShuriken(); return false end
    local stickyWeld = stickyPart:WaitForChild("StickyWeld", 1.5)
    if not stickyWeld then destroyAntiKickShuriken(); return false end
    if not tbl17.sno(stickyPart) then destroyAntiKickShuriken(); return false end
    NinjaShuriken.Name = "ShurikenRSC"
    task.spawn(function()
        local touchInterest = stickyPart:WaitForChild("TouchInterest", 2.5)
        if touchInterest then touchInterest:Destroy() end
    end)
    refreshRemotes()
    if tbl23.spe then
        fireRemote(tbl23.spe, stickyPart, arg, CFrame.new(0, -0.3, 0) * CFrame.Angles(1.5707963267948966, 0, 0))
    end
    local now = tick()
    while true do
        recoveredYield()
        if stickyWeld.Part1 == arg then break
        else
            if not (not stickyWeld.Parent or not arg.Parent or not stickyPart.Parent or not NinjaShuriken or not NinjaShuriken.Parent) then
                local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
                if not (not v24 or v24.Health == 0) then
                    if not (tick() - now > 0.5) then continue end
                end
            end
            break
        end
    end
    return stickyWeld.Parent ~= nil
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.03) do
        if tbl19.AntiKick then
            local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
            if v23 and v24 then
                local ragdollTouchedHitbox = v23:FindFirstChild("RagdollTouchedHitbox")
                if ragdollTouchedHitbox and v24.Health > 0 then
                    local inPlot = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
                    local inOwnedPlot = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot")
                    if inPlot and inPlot.Value then
                        while inPlot.Value and (not inOwnedPlot or not inOwnedPlot.Value) do task.wait() end
                    end
                    if tbl20.AKType == "Shuriken" then
                        if (antiKickAttachedTo(ragdollTouchedHitbox) or attachAntiKickShuriken(ragdollTouchedHitbox)) and NinjaShuriken and NinjaShuriken.Parent then
                            applyAntiKickAppearance()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            destroyAntiKickShuriken()
        end
    end
end)

runPCLDKillLoop = function(arg, arg2)
    repeat
        arg.Health = 0
        pcall(function() arg:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) end)
        recoveredYield()
    until arg2()
end
runPCLDBreakSequence = function(arg)
    local result2 = nil
    if arg.Health <= 0 then else task.spawn(runPCLDKillLoop, arg, function() return result2 ~= nil end) end
    result2 = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local humanoid_ = result2:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if not humanoid_ then return end
    local result3 = nil
    task.spawn(runPCLDKillLoop, humanoid_, function() return result3 ~= nil end)
    result3 = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local humanoid_2 = result3:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if not humanoid_2 then return end
    humanoid_2:SetAttribute("BrokenPCLD", true)
end
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.2) do
        if tbl19.AntiKick2 then
            local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
            if v24 then
                if not v24:GetAttribute("BrokenPCLD") or v24.Health <= 0 then
                    runPCLDBreakSequence(v24)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

    applyAntiPoison = function(arg)

        playerScripts = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("StickyPartsTouchDetection")
        if characterAndBeamMove then characterAndBeamMove.Enabled = not antiLag_ end
        if playerScripts then playerScripts.Enabled = not antiLag_ end
        if antiLag_ then

                for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do

                    if character then

    local n9 = 0
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
        if not tbl19.AutoAntiLag or os.clock() - n9 < 2.5 or deltaTime <= 0 then return end
        n9 = os.clock()
        local antiLagSens = tbl18.AntiLagSens
        if math.floor(1 / deltaTime) < antiLagSens and toggles.AntiLag and not toggles.AntiLag.Value then
            toggles.AntiLag:SetValue(true)
        end
    end)

    local n10 = 0

    local connection4 = nil
    onPossibleKickFlag = function(arg)
        if arg ~= "Flying" then return end
        local now = tick()
        if now - tbl20.LastKickFlagTime <= 1 then tbl20.KickFlags = tbl20.KickFlags + 1
        else tbl20.LastKickFlagTime = now; tbl20.KickFlags = 1 end
        if not tbl19.AutoReset then return end
        if tbl20.KickFlags < 4 then
            local v22, v23, v24 = tbl17.charp()
            if v24 then
                pcall(function()
                    v24:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                    v24:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                end)
            end
        else
            localPlayer:Kick("Resonance (Auto Reset)\nAvoided Kick")
        end
    end
    bindAutoResetRemote = function()
        refreshRemotes()
        local gameCorrectionEvents = v21 and v21:FindFirstChild("GameCorrectionEvents")
        gameCorrectionEvents = gameCorrectionEvents and gameCorrectionEvents:FindFirstChild("GameCorrectionsNotify")
        if not gameCorrectionEvents or not gameCorrectionEvents:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return false end
        if connection4 then connection4:Disconnect() end
        connection4 = gameCorrectionEvents.OnClientEvent:Connect(onPossibleKickFlag)
        return true
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.5) do if bindAutoResetRemote() then break end end
    end)

    local n11 = 0

        n11 += 1

                local v24 = tbl17.charp()
                if v24 then
                    local random = math.random
                    v24:PivotTo(CFrame.new(math.random(-1000, 1000), -7.35, random(-1000, 1000)))
                end

            local v24 = tbl17.charp()
            if v24 then v24:PivotTo(cFrame) end
        end)
    end

    applyDisableVoid = function(destroyVoid)
        tbl19.DestroyVoid = destroyVoid
        if destroyVoid then Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0/0)
        else Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100 end
    end

    applyWaterWalk = function(waterWalk)
        tbl19.WaterWalk = waterWalk
        local alwaysHereTweenedObjects = getMap()
        alwaysHereTweenedObjects = alwaysHereTweenedObjects and alwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
        local ocean = alwaysHereTweenedObjects and alwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("Ocean")
        ocean = ocean and ocean:FindFirstChild("Object")
        ocean = ocean and ocean:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
        if not ocean then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(ocean:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                if waterWalk then
                    child.CanCollide = true
                    if child.Transparency ~= 1 then child.Transparency = 0.5 end
                else
                    child.CanCollide = false
                    if child.Transparency ~= 1 then child.Transparency = 0 end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    applyDeleteBarriers = function(deleteBarriers)
        tbl19.DeleteBarriers = deleteBarriers
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local barrier = child:FindFirstChild("Barrier")
            if barrier then
                for _, child2 in ipairs(barrier:GetChildren()) do
                    if child2:IsA("BasePart") and child2.Name == "PlotBarrier" then
                        child2.CanCollide = not deleteBarriers
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local n12 = 0
    startOtherInvCollisions = function()
        n12 += 1
        local v22 = n12
        task.spawn(function()
            while tbl19.OtherInvCollisions and v22 == n12 and task.wait(0.1) do
                local v23 = tbl17.charp()

                    for _, descendant in ipairs(v23:GetDescendants()) do
                        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                            pcall(function() PhysicsService:SetPartCollisionGroup(descendant, "PlotPlayers") end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            local v23 = tbl17.charp()
            if v23 then
                local str2 = getInOwnedPlotValue() and "PlotPlayers" or "Players"
                for _, descendant in ipairs(v23:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pcall(function() PhysicsService:SetPartCollisionGroup(descendant, str2) end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

    v6:AddButton({ Text = "Break PCLD (Kick Byebye)", Func = function()
        task.spawn(function()
            local tbl16 = {}; local humanoidRootPart = nil; local connection3 = nil; local flag2 = false
            local cframe = CFrame.new(-272.2197265625, -7.3504037857055664, 475.01089477539062)
            Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0/0)
            breakPCLD = function()
                local character = localPlayer.Character
                if not character then return end
                humanoidRootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do

                        tbl16[descendant] = descendant.Part0
                        descendant.Part0 = nil
                    end
                end
                humanoidRootPart.CFrame = cframe
                connection3 = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent then
                        humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end)
            end
            restorePCLD = function()
                if connection3 then connection3:Disconnect(); connection3 = nil end
                for k, v8 in pairs(tbl16) do if k and k.Parent then k.Part0 = v8 end end
                tbl16 = {}
            end
            togglePCLD = function()
                flag2 = not flag2
                if flag2 then breakPCLD() else restorePCLD() end
            end
            togglePCLD(); task.wait(0.12); togglePCLD()
            localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Once(function()
                task.wait(0.25); togglePCLD(); task.wait(0.12); togglePCLD()
            end)
        end)
    end })
end

-- ★ Physics Rep Sync (dy.txt)
local PhysicsGroup = Tabs.Defence:AddLeftGroupbox("Physics")
local physicsConn = nil
local physicsTask = nil

local function stopPhysicsSync()
    if physicsConn then pcall(function() physicsConn:Disconnect() end); physicsConn = nil end
    if physicsTask then pcall(function() task.cancel(physicsTask) end); physicsTask = nil end
end

local function startPhysicsSync()
    stopPhysicsSync()
    physicsTask = task.spawn(function()
        local Player = localPlayer
        local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local HumanoidRootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local WaitFrames = 30
        local Syncing = false
        physicsConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not Character.Parent then
                if physicsConn then physicsConn:Disconnect(); physicsConn = nil end
                return
            end
            if Syncing then return end
            if WaitFrames > 0 then
                pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(HumanoidRootPart, "PhysicsRepRootPart", HumanoidRootPart) end)
                WaitFrames -= 1
            else
                Syncing = true
                pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(HumanoidRootPart, "PhysicsRepRootPart", nil) end)
                task.wait(0.1)
                pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(HumanoidRootPart, "PhysicsRepRootPart", HumanoidRootPart) end)
                WaitFrames = 30
                Syncing = false
            end
        end)
    end)
end

PhysicsGroup:AddToggle("PhysicsRepSync", {
    Text = "Physics Rep Sync",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then startPhysicsSync() else stopPhysicsSync() end
    end
})

-- =====================================================
-- Whitelist タブ
-- =====================================================
_G.ClyrixWhitelist = _G.ClyrixWhitelist or {}

local WhitelistGroup = Tabs.Whitelist:AddLeftGroupbox("Whitelist 追加")
local WhitelistListGroup = Tabs.Whitelist:AddRightGroupbox("Whitelist 一覧")

local whitelistDropdownAdd = nil
local whitelistDropdownRemove = nil
local whitelistLabel = nil

local function getWhitelistPlayerList()
    local list = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
            table.insert(list, player.DisplayName .. " (" .. player.Name .. ")")
        end
    end
    return list
end

local function getWhitelistNames()
    local names = {}
    for name, v in pairs(_G.ClyrixWhitelist) do
        if v then table.insert(names, name) end
    end
    table.sort(names)
    return names
end

local function refreshWhitelistUI()
    if whitelistDropdownAdd then
        pcall(function() whitelistDropdownAdd:SetValues(getWhitelistPlayerList()) end)
    end
    if whitelistDropdownRemove then
        pcall(function() whitelistDropdownRemove:SetValues(getWhitelistNames()) end)
    end
    if whitelistLabel then
        local names = getWhitelistNames()
        local text = "現在のホワイトリスト (" .. #names .. ")\n"
        if #names == 0 then
            text = text .. "(空)"
        else
            for i, n in ipairs(names) do
                text = text .. i .. ". " .. n .. "\n"
            end
        end
        if whitelistLabel.SetText then
            pcall(function() whitelistLabel:SetText(text) end)
        end
    end
end

whitelistDropdownAdd = WhitelistGroup:AddDropdown("WhitelistAddSelect", {
    Text = "プレイヤー選択",
    Default = "",
    Values = getWhitelistPlayerList(),
    Callback = function(v) end,
})

WhitelistGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "ホワイトリストに追加",
    Func = function()
        local val = Options.WhitelistAddSelect and Options.WhitelistAddSelect.Value
        if not val or val == "" then return end
        local name = string.match(val, "%(([^)]+)%)")
        if not name then return end
        _G.ClyrixWhitelist[name] = true
        refreshWhitelistUI()
    end,
})

WhitelistGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "リスト更新",
    Func = function()
        refreshWhitelistUI()
    end,
})

whitelistDropdownRemove = WhitelistListGroup:AddDropdown("WhitelistRemoveSelect", {
    Text = "削除するプレイヤー",
    Default = "",
    Values = getWhitelistNames(),
    Callback = function(v) end,
})

WhitelistListGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "ホワイトリストから削除",
    Func = function()
        local val = Options.WhitelistRemoveSelect and Options.WhitelistRemoveSelect.Value
        if not val or val == "" then return end
        _G.ClyrixWhitelist[val] = nil
        refreshWhitelistUI()
    end,
})

WhitelistListGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "全削除",
    Func = function()
        for k in pairs(_G.ClyrixWhitelist) do
            _G.ClyrixWhitelist[k] = nil
        end
        refreshWhitelistUI()
    end,
})

whitelistLabel = WhitelistListGroup:AddLabel("現在のホワイトリスト (0)\n(空)")

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.5)
    refreshWhitelistUI()
    while task.wait(3) do
        refreshWhitelistUI()
    end
end)
-- =====================================================
-- 通知タブ: LINE通知
-- =====================================================
local LineNotifyGroup = Tabs.Notify:AddLeftGroupbox("LINE通知")

local LineLagCFG = {
    detectorEnabled = true,
    notifyTime = 3,
    timeOut = false,
    lastLineLagger = nil,
}

local LineGrabEvents = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
local LineCreateGrabLine = LineGrabEvents and LineGrabEvents:FindFirstChild("CreateGrabLine")

if LineCreateGrabLine then
    LineCreateGrabLine.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(arg, arg2, arg3)
        if not LineLagCFG.detectorEnabled then return end
        if LineLagCFG.timeOut then return end
        if typeof(arg3) ~= "CFrame" then return end

        local spawnLoc = Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation")
        if not spawnLoc then return end

        if (spawnLoc.Position - arg3.Position).Magnitude > 100000 then
            local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(arg))
            if not plr then return end

            LineLagCFG.timeOut = true

            if LineLagCFG.lastLineLagger ~= plr then
                LineLagCFG.lastLineLagger = plr

                local notifyText = "Player: " .. plr.DisplayName .. " (" .. plr.Name .. ")"
                .. "\nCFrame: ("
                    .. math.round(arg3.Position.X) .. ", "
                    .. math.round(arg3.Position.Y) .. ", "
                    .. math.round(arg3.Position.Z) .. ")"

                ClyrixNotify("⚠ Line Lag Detected", notifyText, LineLagCFG.notifyTime)
            end

            task.wait(LineLagCFG.timeOut and 3.3 or 0)
            LineLagCFG.timeOut = false
            LineLagCFG.lastLineLagger = nil
        end
    end)
end

LineNotifyGroup:AddToggle("LineLagDetectorToggle", {
    Text = "Line Lag Detector",
    Default = true,
    Callback = function(v)
        LineLagCFG.detectorEnabled = v
    end,
})

LineNotifyGroup:AddSlider("LineLagNotifyTime", {
    Text = "通知の表示時間",
    Default = 3, Min = 1, Max = 10, Rounding = 0, Suffix = "秒",
    Callback = function(v)
        LineLagCFG.notifyTime = v
    end,
})
-- =====================================================
-- 通知タブ: Kick通知
-- =====================================================
local KickNotifyGroup = Tabs.Notify:AddLeftGroupbox("Kick通知")

local KickNotifyState = {
    enabled = false,
    watcherConn = nil,
    lastKickLabel = nil,
}

local KICK_WATCHED_NAMES = {
    ["BlackHoleKick"] = true,
    ["BlackHoleDetected"] = true,
}

local KICK_SOUND_ID = "rbxassetid://2865227271"
local KICK_SOUND_VOLUME = 0.5

local function kickPlaySound()
    local s = Instance.new("Sound")
    s.SoundId = KICK_SOUND_ID
    s.Volume = KICK_SOUND_VOLUME
    s.PlayOnRemove = true
    s.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
    s:Destroy()
end

local function kickGetClosestPlayer(pos)
    local closestPlr = nil
    local closestDist = math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
            local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                local dist = (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude
                if dist < closestDist then
                    closestDist = dist
                    closestPlr = plr
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closestPlr
end

local function kickStartWatcher()
    if KickNotifyState.watcherConn then
        pcall(function() KickNotifyState.watcherConn:Disconnect() end)
        KickNotifyState.watcherConn = nil
    end
    KickNotifyState.watcherConn = Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not KickNotifyState.enabled then return end
        if not KICK_WATCHED_NAMES[obj.Name] then return end
        task.wait(0.05)
        local pos
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            pos = obj.Position
        elseif obj:IsA("Model") and obj.PrimaryPart then
            pos = obj.PrimaryPart.Position
        end
        if not pos then return end
        local plr = kickGetClosestPlayer(pos)
        if not plr then return end
        kickPlaySound()
        ClyrixNotify("KickNotify",
            "Player: " .. plr.DisplayName .. " (" .. plr.Name .. ")"
            .. "\nKicked!",
        6)
        if KickNotifyState.lastKickLabel and KickNotifyState.lastKickLabel.SetText then
            pcall(function()
                KickNotifyState.lastKickLabel:SetText("Last kick: " .. plr.DisplayName)
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function kickStopWatcher()
    if KickNotifyState.watcherConn then
        pcall(function() KickNotifyState.watcherConn:Disconnect() end)
        KickNotifyState.watcherConn = nil
    end
end

KickNotifyGroup:AddToggle("KickNotifyToggle", {
    Text = "Kick Notify",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        KickNotifyState.enabled = v
        if v then
            kickStartWatcher()
        else
            kickStopWatcher()
        end
    end,
})

KickNotifyState.lastKickLabel = KickNotifyGroup:AddLabel("Last kick: —")

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if KickNotifyState.enabled then
        task.wait(0.5)
        kickStartWatcher()
    end
end)

-- =====================================================
-- Server タブ: HOUSE (Plot Breaker)
-- =====================================================
local HouseGroup = Tabs.Server:AddLeftGroupbox("HOUSE")

local function runPlotBreaker()
    local inv = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
    if not inv then return end
    local currentHRP
    if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        currentHRP = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
    else
        local character = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        currentHRP = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    if not currentHRP then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local shur = nil
            local conn = inv.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name == "NinjaShuriken" then
                    shur = child
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                replicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("NinjaShuriken", currentHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(5, 8, 20), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
            end)
            local startTime = tick()
            repeat
                if shur and shur:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and shur:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then break end
                task.wait(0.01)
            until tick() - startTime > 0.5
            shur = shur or inv:FindFirstChild("NinjaShuriken")
            if shur then
                local soundPart = shur:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                local stickyPart = shur:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                if soundPart and stickyPart then
                    local setOwner = replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")
                    for j = 1, 15 do
                        pcall(function() setOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame) end)
                        if soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and soundPart.PartOwner.Value == LocalPlayer.Name then break end
                    end
                    for _, obj in pairs(shur:GetChildren()) do
                        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                            obj.CanTouch = false
                            obj.CanCollide = false
                            obj.Transparency = 1
                        end
                    end
                    shur.Name = "Noclipped"
                    local plot = Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. i)
                    if plot then
                        local plotArea = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotArea")
                        if plotArea then
                            pcall(function()
                                replicatedStorage.PlayerEvents.StickyPartEvent:FireServer(stickyPart, plotArea, CFrame.new(1e12, 1e12, 1e12))
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end

HouseGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "Break All Plots (1-5)",
    Func = function()
        runPlotBreaker()
    end
})

local NoBlobSettingsGroup = Tabs.Server:AddLeftGroupbox("ノーブロブセッティング")

local DestroyLineCount = 39
local DestroySelectedHeight = "Spawn"
local DestroyCustomHeight = 35
local DestroyHeavenHeight = 1e9
local DestroyLineLagEnabled = false
local DestroyOwnerSpamCount = 2
local DestroyOwnerSpamInterval = 0.01
local DestroyRadius = 85
local DestroyGrabEvents = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")

    Text = "破壊の高さモード",

    Callback = function(v)
        if v == "Heaven (天国)" then
            DestroySelectedHeight = "Heaven"
        elseif v == "Custom (カスタム)" then
            DestroySelectedHeight = "Custom"
        else
            DestroySelectedHeight = "Spawn"
        end
    end,
})

    Text = "破壊の高さ (Custom時)",

})

})

    Text = "配置半径",
    Default = 85, Min = 5, Max = 300, Rounding = 0, Suffix = " studs",
    Callback = function(v) DestroyRadius = v end,
})
