    Text = "オーナースパム回数",
    Default = 2, Min = 1, Max = 20, Rounding = 0, Suffix = " 回",
    Callback = function(v) DestroyOwnerSpamCount = v end,
})

NoBlobSettingsGroup:AddSlider("DestroyOwnerSpamInterval", {
    Text = "オーナースパム間隔",
    Default = 0.01, Min = 0.01, Max = 1, Rounding = 2, Suffix = " 秒",
    Callback = function(v) DestroyOwnerSpamInterval = v end,
})

NoBlobGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "ServerDestroy",
    Func = function()
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                local h
                if DestroySelectedHeight == "Heaven" then
                    h = DestroyHeavenHeight
                elseif DestroySelectedHeight == "Custom" then
                    h = DestroyCustomHeight
                else
                    h = 35
                end

                DestroyLineLagEnabled = true
                task.spawn(function()
                    while DestroyLineLagEnabled do
                        pcall(function()
                            local cl = DestroyGrabEvents and DestroyGrabEvents:FindFirstChild("CreateGrabLine")
                            if cl then
                                local sl = Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation")
                                    or Workspace:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                                    or (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                                if sl then
                                    for i = 1, DestroyLineCount do
                                        cl:FireServer(sl, CFrame.new(math.random(-1e9, 1e9), 0, math.random(-1e9, 1e9)))
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(0.01)
                    end
                end)
                task.wait(1)

                local ps = {}
                for _, plr in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer and not (_G.ClyrixWhitelist and _G.ClyrixWhitelist[plr.Name]) then
                table.insert(ps, plr)
                   end
                end
                if #ps == 0 then
                    DestroyLineLagEnabled = false
                    return
                end

                local myHrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not myHrp then DestroyLineLagEnabled = false; return end

                local pd = {}
                for _, plr in ipairs(ps) do
                    local hrp = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then table.insert(pd, { player = plr, hrp = hrp }) end
                end

                for _, d in ipairs(pd) do
                    pcall(function()
                        myHrp.CFrame = d.hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 5)
                        myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.2)
                    if DestroyGrabEvents then
                        local so = DestroyGrabEvents:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")
                        if so then
                            for i = 1, DestroyOwnerSpamCount do
                                pcall(function() so:FireServer(d.hrp, d.hrp.CFrame) end)
                                pcall(function() so:FireServer(d.hrp) end)
                                task.wait(DestroyOwnerSpamInterval)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end

                local step = (math.pi * 2) / #pd
                for i, d in ipairs(pd) do
                    local a = (i - 1) * step
                    local x, z = math.cos(a) * DestroyRadius, math.sin(a) * DestroyRadius
                    pcall(function()
                        d.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, h, z)
                        d.hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end)
                    local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                    bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e19, 1e19, 1e19)
                    bp.P = 1e19
                    bp.Position = Vector3.new(x, h, z)
                    bp.Parent = d.hrp
                    task.delay(2, function()
                        pcall(function() bp:Destroy() end)
                    end)
                    task.wait()
                end

                for _ = 1, 8 do
                    for _, d in ipairs(pd) do
                        pcall(function()
                            local cl = DestroyGrabEvents:FindFirstChild("CreateGrabLine")
                            local dl = DestroyGrabEvents:FindFirstChild("DestroyGrabLine")
                            if cl and dl then
                                cl:FireServer(d.hrp, CFrame.new(0, 1e9, 0))
                                task.wait()
                                dl:FireServer(d.hrp)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.3)
                end
            end)
        end)
    end,
})

NoBlobGroup:AddToggle("DestroyLineLagToggle", {
    Text = "ラインラグを単体で実行",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        DestroyLineLagEnabled = v
        if v then
            task.spawn(function()
                while DestroyLineLagEnabled do
                    pcall(function()
                        local cl = DestroyGrabEvents and DestroyGrabEvents:FindFirstChild("CreateGrabLine")
                        if cl then
                            local sl = Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation")
                                or Workspace:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                                or (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                            if sl then
                                for i = 1, DestroyLineCount do
                                    cl:FireServer(sl, CFrame.new(math.random(-1e9, 1e9), 0, math.random(-1e9, 1e9)))
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.01)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})
NoBlobGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "Stop Lag",
    Func = function()
        DestroyLineLagEnabled = false
        if Toggles.DestroyLineLagToggle then
            pcall(function() Toggles.DestroyLineLagToggle:SetValue(false) end)
        end
    end,
})
-- =====================================================
-- Server タブ: Blobman (右側・ノーブロブの下)
-- =====================================================
ClyrixBlobGroup = Tabs.Server:AddRightGroupbox("Blobman", "user")

ClyrixBlobLastSeatNotify = 0
ClyrixBlobCurrent = nil
ClyrixBlobKillRunning = false
ClyrixBlobKickAllRunning = false

function ClyrixBlobRequireSeated()
    local ok = false
    pcall(function()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local seat = hum and hum.SeatPart
        if seat and seat:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
            local obj = seat
            while obj do
                if obj:IsA("Model") and obj.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                    ok = true
                    return
                end
                obj = obj.Parent
            end
        end
    end)
    if ok then return true end
    if tick() - ClyrixBlobLastSeatNotify > 2 then
        ClyrixBlobLastSeatNotify = tick()
        ClyrixNotify("Blobman", "Please sit on Blobman first", 3)
    end
    return false
end

function ClyrixBlobIsTargetable(player)
    if not player or player == LocalPlayer then return false end
    if _G.ClyrixWhitelist and _G.ClyrixWhitelist[player.Name] then return false end
    return true
end

function ClyrixBlobGetSeatedBlobman()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return nil end
    local seat = humanoid.SeatPart
    if not seat or not seat:IsA("VehicleSeat") then return nil end
    local obj = seat
    while obj do
        if obj:IsA("Model") and obj.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
            return obj
        end
        obj = obj.Parent
    end
    return nil
end

function ClyrixBlobSpawn()
    local seated = ClyrixBlobGetSeatedBlobman()
    if seated then
        ClyrixBlobCurrent = seated
        return seated
    end
    if ClyrixBlobCurrent and ClyrixBlobCurrent.Parent then
        return ClyrixBlobCurrent
    end
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character then return nil end
    local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not rootPart then return nil end
    local spawnPos = rootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5)
    pcall(function()
        replicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("CreatureBlobman", spawnPos, Vector3.new(0, 127, 0))
    end)
    local toyFolderName = LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"
    local blobman = nil
    local startTime = tick()
    repeat
        local toyFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(toyFolderName)
        if toyFolder then
            blobman = toyFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
        end
        if blobman then break end
        task.wait()
    until tick() - startTime > 2
    if not blobman then return nil end
    ClyrixBlobCurrent = blobman
    return blobman
end

function ClyrixBlobKillPlayer(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer or not targetPlayer.Character then return false end
    local humanoid = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return false end
    for _ = 1, 5 do
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
        end)
        if humanoid.Health <= 0 then return true end
        task.wait(0.01)
    end
    return false
end

function ClyrixBlobGrabRelease(blobman, targetRoot)
    if not blobman or not targetRoot then return end
    pcall(function()
        local script = blobman:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
        if script then
            script.CreatureGrab:FireServer(blobman.LeftDetector, targetRoot, blobman.LeftDetector.LeftWeld)
            script.CreatureRelease:FireServer(blobman.LeftDetector.LeftWeld)
        end
    end)
end

function ClyrixBlobKillAll()
    if not ClyrixBlobRequireSeated() then return end
    local localChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not localChar or not localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
    local rootPart = localChar.HumanoidRootPart
    local blobman = ClyrixBlobSpawn()
    if not blobman then return end
    local targets = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if ClyrixBlobIsTargetable(player) and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            table.insert(targets, player)
        end
    end
    if #targets == 0 then
        ClyrixNotify("Blobman", "No targets (whitelist active)", 3)
        return
    end
    for _, player in ipairs(targets) do
        if not ClyrixBlobKillRunning then break end
        local targetChar = player.Character
        if not targetChar then continue end
        local humanoid = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then continue end
        local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not targetRoot then continue end
        rootPart.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame
        task.wait(0.02)
        ClyrixBlobKillPlayer(player)
        for _ = 1, 2 do
            ClyrixBlobGrabRelease(blobman, targetRoot)
            task.wait(0.01)
        end
    end
end

function ClyrixBlobKickAll()
    if ClyrixBlobKickAllRunning then return end
    if not ClyrixBlobRequireSeated() then return end
    ClyrixBlobKickAllRunning = true
    local allPlayers = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if ClyrixBlobIsTargetable(player) then
            table.insert(allPlayers, player)
        end
    end
    if #allPlayers == 0 then
        ClyrixNotify("Blobman", "No targets (whitelist active)", 3)
        ClyrixBlobKickAllRunning = false
        return
    end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local rootPart = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if rootPart then
        local spawnPos = rootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5)
        pcall(function()
            replicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("CreatureBlobman", spawnPos, Vector3.new(0, 127, 0))
        end)
    end
    task.wait(0.5)
    local toyFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    local currentBlob = toyFolder and toyFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
    if not currentBlob then
        ClyrixBlobKickAllRunning = false
        return
    end
    local vehicleSeat = currentBlob:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
    if vehicleSeat and LocalPlayer.Character then
        pcall(function()
            vehicleSeat:Sit(LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
        end)
    end
    task.wait(0.3)
    local myRoot = rootPart
    if not myRoot then
        ClyrixBlobKickAllRunning = false
        return
    end
    for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(allPlayers) do
        local targetRoot = targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if targetRoot then
            myRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame
            task.wait(0.02)
            for i = 1, 3 do
                pcall(function()
                    currentBlob.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureGrab:FireServer(
                        currentBlob.LeftDetector, targetRoot, currentBlob.LeftDetector.LeftWeld
                    )
                    currentBlob.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureRelease:FireServer(currentBlob.LeftDetector.LeftWeld)
                end)
                if i < 3 then task.wait(0.08) end
            end
        end
    end
    myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 100, 0)
    task.wait(0.1)
    local radius = 15
    for i, targetPlayer in ipairs(allPlayers) do
        local targetRoot = targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if targetRoot then
            local angle = math.rad((i - 1) * (360 / #allPlayers))
            local x = radius * math.cos(angle)
            local z = radius * math.sin(angle)
            targetRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, 110, z)
        end
    end
    task.wait(0.1)
    for _ = 1, 2 do
        for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(allPlayers) do
            local targetRoot = targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if targetRoot then
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        replicatedStorage.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(targetRoot, CFrame.new(targetRoot.Position))
                        replicatedStorage.GrabEvents.DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(targetRoot)
                    end)
                end)
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    task.wait(0.3)
    for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(allPlayers) do
        local targetRoot = targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if targetRoot then
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    currentBlob.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureGrab:FireServer(
                        currentBlob.LeftDetector, targetRoot, currentBlob.LeftDetector.LeftWeld
                    )
                    currentBlob.BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript.CreatureGrab:FireServer(
                        currentBlob.RightDetector, targetRoot, currentBlob.RightDetector.RightWeld
                    )
                end)
            end)
        end
    end
    task.wait(0.1)
    myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -50000, 0)
    for _, part in ipairs(currentBlob:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then pcall(function() part.Anchored = false end) end
    end
    task.wait(1)
    ClyrixBlobKickAllRunning = false
end

ClyrixBlobGroup:AddToggle("ClyrixBlobKillAllToggle", {
    Text = "Blobman Kill All",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        ClyrixBlobKillRunning = v
        if v then
            task.spawn(function()
                while ClyrixBlobKillRunning do
                    ClyrixBlobKillAll()
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

ClyrixBlobGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "Blobman Kick All",
    Func = function()
        task.spawn(ClyrixBlobKickAll)
    end,
})
-- =====================================================
-- Visuals: Image ESP
-- =====================================================
local EspGroup = Tabs.Visuals:AddLeftGroupbox("ESP", "eye")

local ESP_CONFIG = { ImageId = "rbxassetid://85976003217279", MaxDistance = 500, BaseSize = 10 }
local ESP_ROTATE_SPEED = 160
local ESP_PULSE_SPEED = 1.8
local ESP_PULSE_AMOUNT = 0.35
local ESP_COLOR_LARGE = Color3.fromRGB(130, 60, 255)
local ESP_COLOR_SMALL = Color3.fromRGB(220, 40, 40)
local ESP_STATE = { Enabled = false }
local espData = {}

local function shouldShow(player)
    if not ESP_STATE.Enabled then return false end
    if player == LocalPlayer then return false end
    return true
end

local function removeESP(player)
    local data = espData[player]
    if not data then return end
    if data.Billboard then data.Billboard:Destroy() end
    espData[player] = nil
end

local function createESP(player)
    removeESP(player)
    if not shouldShow(player) then return end
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "ClyrixESP_Image"
    billboard.Adornee = root
    billboard.Size = UDim2.fromScale(ESP_CONFIG.BaseSize, ESP_CONFIG.BaseSize)
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.Parent = char
    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
    container.Name = "Container"
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    container.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    container.Parent = billboard
    local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    img.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    img.Image = ESP_CONFIG.ImageId
    img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    img.ImageColor3 = ESP_COLOR_LARGE
    img.Parent = container
    espData[player] = { Billboard = billboard, Container = container, Image = img, BaseSize = ESP_CONFIG.BaseSize }
end

local function espRefreshAll()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do createESP(player) end
end

local function espWatchPlayer(player)
    if player == LocalPlayer then return end
    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.1)
        createESP(player)
    end)
    player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function() removeESP(player) end)
    if player.Character then createESP(player) end
end

for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do espWatchPlayer(player) end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(espWatchPlayer)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removeESP)

EspGroup:AddToggle("ImageEspToggle", { Text = "Image ESP", Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value) ESP_STATE.Enabled = Value; espRefreshAll() end })
EspGroup:AddSlider("EspMaxDistance", { Text = "Max Distance", Default = 500, Min = 50, Max = 2000, Rounding = 0, Suffix = " studs",
    Callback = function(Value) ESP_CONFIG.MaxDistance = Value end })
EspGroup:AddSlider("EspBaseSize", { Text = "Base Size", Default = 10, Min = 1, Max = 50, Rounding = 1,
    Callback = function(Value)
        ESP_CONFIG.BaseSize = Value
        for _, data in pairs(espData) do data.BaseSize = Value end
    end })

-- PCLD ESP (ESP Groupbox 内)
local PCLDEnabled = false
local TweenService_PCLD = game:GetService("TweenService")

local function PCLD_ApplyCloned(clonedPCLD)
    pcall(function()
        if PCLDEnabled then
            clonedPCLD.Transparency = 0.7
            clonedPCLD.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            local sb = clonedPCLD:FindFirstChild("SelectionBox")
            if not sb then
                sb = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
                sb.Adornee = clonedPCLD
                sb.Parent = clonedPCLD
            end
            sb.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            sb.LineThickness = 0.01
        else
            clonedPCLD.Transparency = 1
            local sb = clonedPCLD:FindFirstChild("SelectionBox")
            if sb then sb:Destroy() end
        end
    end)
end

local function PCLD_UpdateParent(parent)
    if parent.Name ~= "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        if parent.Position == Vector3.zero then
            while parent and parent.Position == Vector3.zero do
                task.wait()
            end
        end
        if not parent.Parent then return end
        local clonedPCLD = parent:FindFirstChild("ClonedPCLD")
        if PCLDEnabled and not clonedPCLD then
            clonedPCLD = parent:Clone()
            clonedPCLD.Name = "ClonedPCLD"
            clonedPCLD.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            clonedPCLD.Parent = parent
            parent:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CFrame"):Connect(function()
                if not clonedPCLD.Parent then return end
                pcall(function()
                    TweenService_PCLD:Create(
                        clonedPCLD,
                        TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
                        { CFrame = parent.CFrame }
                    ):Play()
                end)
            end)
        end
        if clonedPCLD then
            PCLD_ApplyCloned(clonedPCLD)
        end
    end)
end

local function PCLD_UpdateAll()
    for _, v in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
        PCLD_UpdateParent(v)
    end
end

Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if PCLDEnabled then
        PCLD_UpdateParent(child)
    end
end)

EspGroup:AddToggle("PCLDViewToggle", {
    Text = "PCLD View",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(value)
        PCLDEnabled = value
        if value then
            PCLD_UpdateAll()
        else
            for _, v in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if v.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" then
                    local clone = v:FindFirstChild("ClonedPCLD")
                    if clone then
                        clone.Transparency = 1
                        local sb = clone:FindFirstChild("SelectionBox")
                        if sb then sb:Destroy() end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end,
})

task.spawn(function()
    local accumulatedTime = 0
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        accumulatedTime += dt
        for _, data in pairs(espData) do
            if data.Billboard and data.Container and data.Image then
                data.Container.Rotation = (data.Container.Rotation + ESP_ROTATE_SPEED * dt) % 360
                local base = data.BaseSize or ESP_CONFIG.BaseSize
                local factor = 1 + math.sin(accumulatedTime * (math.pi * 2 / ESP_PULSE_SPEED)) * ESP_PULSE_AMOUNT
                local t = (factor - (1 - ESP_PULSE_AMOUNT)) / (2 * ESP_PULSE_AMOUNT)
                t = math.clamp(t, 0, 1)
                local color = ESP_COLOR_SMALL:Lerp(ESP_COLOR_LARGE, t)
                local size = base * factor
                data.Billboard.Size = UDim2.fromScale(size, size)
                data.Image.ImageColor3 = color
            end
        end
    end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)
        local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if myRoot then
            for player, data in pairs(espData) do
                local char = player.Character
                local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root and data.Billboard then
                    local dist = (myRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                    local visible = dist <= ESP_CONFIG.MaxDistance and ESP_STATE.Enabled
                    data.Billboard.Enabled = visible
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- =====================================================
-- Visuals: Black Hole Texture
-- =====================================================
local BlackHoleGroup = Tabs.Visuals:AddLeftGroupbox("Black Hole Texture")

local BHK_Master = {
    enabled = false,
    watcherConn = nil,
    appliedList = {},
    selectedTexture = "blackhole1",
}

local BHK_Textures = {
    blackhole1 = {
        assetId = "rbxassetid://99340233207059",
        display = "blackhole1",
        image = "rbxassetid://99340233207059",
    },
    blackhole2 = {
        assetId = "rbxassetid://8387416709",
        display = "blackhole2",
        image = "rbxassetid://8387416709",
    },
    blackhole3 = {
        assetId = "rbxassetid://8777548884",
        display = "blackhole3",
        image = "rbxassetid://8777548884",
    },
}

local function BHK_applyRealisticBH(model, assetId)
    if not model then return end
    local success, realisticModel = pcall(function()
        return game:GetObjects(assetId)[1]
    end)
    if not success or not realisticModel then return end
    local hole = model:FindFirstChild("Hole")
    if not hole then
        realisticModel:Destroy()
        return
    end
    for _, obj in pairs(realisticModel:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Sound") or obj:IsA("Trail") then
            local clone = obj:Clone()
            clone.Parent = hole
            table.insert(BHK_Master.appliedList, clone)
        end
        if obj:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            local currentGui = hole:FindFirstChild("BillboardGui")
            if currentGui then
                local newGui = obj:Clone()
                newGui.Parent = hole
                if currentGui:FindFirstChild("Large") and newGui:FindFirstChild("Large") then
                    currentGui.Large.Image = newGui.Large.Image
                end
                if currentGui:FindFirstChild("Small") and newGui:FindFirstChild("Small") then
                    currentGui.Small.Image = newGui.Small.Image
                end
                newGui:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    realisticModel:Destroy()
end

local function BHK_applyAll(model)
    if not model then return end
    local tex = BHK_Textures[BHK_Master.selectedTexture]
    if tex then
        BHK_applyRealisticBH(model, tex.assetId)
    end
end

local function BHK_startAll()
    BHK_Master.enabled = true
    BHK_Master.appliedList = {}
    local existing = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BlackHoleKick")
    if existing then
        BHK_applyAll(existing)
    end
    if BHK_Master.watcherConn then
        pcall(function() BHK_Master.watcherConn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    BHK_Master.watcherConn = Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "BlackHoleKick" then
            task.wait(0.1)
            BHK_applyAll(child)
        end
    end)
end

local function BHK_stopAll()
    BHK_Master.enabled = false
    if BHK_Master.watcherConn then
        pcall(function() BHK_Master.watcherConn:Disconnect() end)
        BHK_Master.watcherConn = nil
    end
    for _, obj in ipairs(BHK_Master.appliedList) do
        pcall(function()
            if obj and obj.Parent then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
    BHK_Master.appliedList = {}
end

BlackHoleGroup:AddDropdown("BlackHoleTextureSelect", {
    Text = "テクスチャ選択",
    Values = { "blackhole1", "blackhole2", "blackhole3" },
    Default = "blackhole1",
    Callback = function(v)
        BHK_Master.selectedTexture = v
        if BHK_Master.enabled then
            BHK_stopAll()
            task.wait(0.1)
            BHK_startAll()
        end
    end,
})

BlackHoleGroup:AddToggle("BlackHoleToggle", {
    Text = "ブラックホールテクスチャ",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            BHK_startAll()
        else
            BHK_stopAll()
        end
    end,
})

-- ドロップダウン項目に画像を追加
task.spawn(function()
    local function tryInject()
        local root = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
        for _, desc in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("TextButton") and BHK_Textures[desc.Text] then
    local parent = desc.Parent
    -- ドロップダウンのオプションリスト（ScrollingFrame内）のみ対象
    if parent and parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
        if not desc:FindFirstChild("ClyrixBHIcon") then
            local tex = BHK_Textures[desc.Text]
            local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            icon.Name = "ClyrixBHIcon"
            icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
            icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -9)
            icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            icon.Image = tex.image
            icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            icon.Parent = desc

            -- テキストを右にずらす
            local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
            padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 28)
            padding.Parent = desc
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    while task.wait(0.3) do
        pcall(tryInject)
    end
end)
-- =====================================================
-- Visuals: Time (ワールド時間変更)
-- =====================================================
ClyrixTimeLighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
ClyrixTimeOriginalClock = ClyrixTimeLighting.ClockTime
ClyrixTimeOriginalOfDay = ClyrixTimeLighting.TimeOfDay
ClyrixTimeEnabled = false
ClyrixTimeHour = 12
ClyrixTimeMinute = 0

ClyrixTimeGroup = Tabs.Visuals:AddRightGroupbox("Time", "clock")

ClyrixTimeGroup:AddToggle("TimeEnable", {
    Text = "時間変更を有効化",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(value)
        ClyrixTimeEnabled = value
        if value then
            ClyrixTimeOriginalClock = ClyrixTimeLighting.ClockTime
            ClyrixTimeOriginalOfDay = ClyrixTimeLighting.TimeOfDay
        else
            ClyrixTimeLighting.ClockTime = ClyrixTimeOriginalClock
            ClyrixTimeLighting.TimeOfDay = ClyrixTimeOriginalOfDay
        end
    end,
})

ClyrixTimeGroup:AddDropdown("TimePreset", {
    Text = "プリセット",
    Values = {
        "朝 (06:00)",
        "昼 (12:00)",
        "夕方 (18:00)",
        "夜 (00:00)",
        "深夜 (03:00)",
    },
    Default = 2,
    Callback = function(value)
        if value == "朝 (06:00)" then
            ClyrixTimeHour = 6
        elseif value == "昼 (12:00)" then
            ClyrixTimeHour = 12
        elseif value == "夕方 (18:00)" then
            ClyrixTimeHour = 18
        elseif value == "夜 (00:00)" then
            ClyrixTimeHour = 0
        elseif value == "深夜 (03:00)" then
            ClyrixTimeHour = 3
        end
        ClyrixTimeMinute = 0
        if ClyrixTimeEnabled then
            ClyrixTimeLighting.ClockTime = ClyrixTimeHour + ClyrixTimeMinute / 60
        end
    end,
})

ClyrixTimeGroup:AddSlider("TimeHour", {
    Text = "時 (Hour)",
    Default = 12, Min = 0, Max = 23, Rounding = 0, Suffix = "時",
    Callback = function(value)
        ClyrixTimeHour = value
        if ClyrixTimeEnabled then
            ClyrixTimeLighting.ClockTime = ClyrixTimeHour + ClyrixTimeMinute / 60
        end
    end,
})

ClyrixTimeGroup:AddSlider("TimeMinute", {
    Text = "分 (Minute)",
    Default = 0, Min = 0, Max = 59, Rounding = 0, Suffix = "分",
    Callback = function(value)
        ClyrixTimeMinute = value
        if ClyrixTimeEnabled then
            ClyrixTimeLighting.ClockTime = ClyrixTimeHour + ClyrixTimeMinute / 60
        end
    end,
})

ClyrixTimeGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "リセット（元の時間に戻す）",
    Func = function()
        ClyrixTimeEnabled = false
        pcall(function() Toggles.TimeEnable:SetValue(false) end)
        ClyrixTimeLighting.ClockTime = ClyrixTimeOriginalClock
        ClyrixTimeLighting.TimeOfDay = ClyrixTimeOriginalOfDay
    end,
})
-- =====================================================
-- Fun タブ: Poses (おんぶ.txt ソース統合)
-- =====================================================
function InitPosesTab()
    -- ============================================================
    -- Services
    -- ============================================================
    local PosesPlayers = game:GetService("Players")
    local PosesReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local PosesRunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local PosesWorkspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

    local PosesLocalPlayer = PosesPlayers.LocalPlayer
    local PosesGrabEvents = PosesReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents")
    local PosesSetNetworkOwner = PosesGrabEvents:WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")

    local posesChar = PosesLocalPlayer.Character or PosesLocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local posesToysFolder = PosesWorkspace:FindFirstChild(PosesLocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

    PosesLocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
        posesChar = c
        task.wait(0.25)
        posesToysFolder = PosesWorkspace:WaitForChild(PosesLocalPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys", 5)
    end)

    -- ============================================================
    -- Helper Functions (original source)
    -- ============================================================
    local function PosesSpawnToy(toyName, cframe)
        task.spawn(function()
            PosesReplicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(toyName, cframe, Vector3.zero)
        end)
    end

    local function PosesGetPlot()
        if not PosesWorkspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(PosesLocalPlayer.Name) then
            return false
        end
        for _, plot in PosesWorkspace.Plots:GetChildren() do
            local plotSign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local thisPlotsOwners = plotSign and plotSign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
            if thisPlotsOwners then
                for _, owner in thisPlotsOwners:GetChildren() do
                    if owner.Value == PosesLocalPlayer.Name then
                        local plotItems = PosesWorkspace.PlotItems:FindFirstChild(plot.Name)
                        if plotItems then
                            return true, plotItems
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function PosesDestroyT(toy)
        PosesReplicatedStorage.MenuToys.DestroyToy:FireServer(toy or posesToysFolder:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Model"))
    end

    -- ============================================================
    -- State Variables (original source)
    -- ============================================================
    local selectedPlayerForPoses = nil
    local foodPlate = nil
    local targetCharacter = nil
    local loopActive = true
    local useClone = false

    local n13, n14, n15 = 0, 1.5, 0
    local n16, n17, n18 = 0, 10, 0
    local n19, n20, n21 = -1.5, 0, 0
    local n22, n23, n24 = 1.5, 0, 0
    local n25, n26, n27 = -0.5, -2, 0
    local n28, n29, n30 = 0.5, -2, 0
    local n31, n32, n33 = 0, 0, 0
    local n34, n35, n36 = 0, 0, 0
    local n37, n38, n39 = 0, 0, 0
    local n40, n41, n42 = 0, 0, 0
    local n43, n44, n45 = 0, 0, 0
    local n46, n47, n48 = 0, 0, 0

    -- ============================================================
    -- UI
    -- ============================================================
    local PosesSettingGroup = Tabs.Fun:AddLeftGroupbox("setting", "settings")
    local PosesTargetGroup = Tabs.Fun:AddRightGroupbox("target", "target")

    local poseSliders = {}

    -- HEAD
    PosesSettingGroup:AddDivider("Head")
    poseSliders.HeadRotX    = PosesSettingGroup:AddSlider("SliderHeadRotX",    {Text = "Head Rot X",    Default = 0,   Min = -180, Max = 180, Rounding = 0, Callback = function(v) n31 = v end})

    poseSliders.RLOffsetX = PosesSettingGroup:AddSlider("SliderRLOffsetX", {Text = "Right Leg Offset X", Default = 0.5, Min = -10,  Max = 10,  Rounding = 1, Callback = function(v) n28 = v end})
    poseSliders.RLOffsetY = PosesSettingGroup:AddSlider("SliderRLOffsetY", {Text = "Right Leg Offset Y", Default = -2,  Min = -10,  Max = 10,  Rounding = 1, Callback = function(v) n29 = v end})
    poseSliders.RLOffsetZ = PosesSettingGroup:AddSlider("SliderRLOffsetZ", {Text = "Right Leg Offset Z", Default = 0,   Min = -10,  Max = 10,  Rounding = 1, Callback = function(v) n30 = v end})

    -- ============================================================
    -- Target Groupbox
    -- ============================================================
    local function PosesGetPlayerList()
        local list = {}
        for _, p in PosesPlayers:GetPlayers() do
            if p ~= PosesLocalPlayer then
                table.insert(list, p.DisplayName .. " [" .. p.Name .. "]")
            end
        end
        return list
    end

    PosesTargetGroup:AddDropdown("SelectPosePlayer", {
        Text = "Select Player",
        Default = "",
        Values = PosesGetPlayerList(),
        Callback = function(option)
            local val = option
            if val and val ~= "" then
                local match = tostring(val):match("%[(.-)%]")
                if match then
                    selectedPlayerForPoses = PosesPlayers:FindFirstChild(match)
                end
            else
                selectedPlayerForPoses = nil
            end
        end,
    })

    PosesTargetGroup:AddDropdown("PosesPreset", {
        Text = "Poses",
        Default = "HeadSit",
        Values = { "HeadSit", "Penis", "Blowjob" },
        Callback = function(sel)
            if sel == "HeadSit" then
                poseSliders.HeadOffsetX:SetValue(0);   poseSliders.HeadOffsetY:SetValue(1.5); poseSliders.HeadOffsetZ:SetValue(-0.2)
                poseSliders.TorsoOffsetX:SetValue(0);  poseSliders.TorsoOffsetY:SetValue(2);  poseSliders.TorsoOffsetZ:SetValue(0.75)
                poseSliders.LAOffsetX:SetValue(-1);    poseSliders.LAOffsetY:SetValue(0.4);   poseSliders.LAOffsetZ:SetValue(-0.75)
                poseSliders.RAOffsetX:SetValue(1);     poseSliders.RAOffsetY:SetValue(0.3);   poseSliders.RAOffsetZ:SetValue(-0.75)
                poseSliders.LLOffsetX:SetValue(-0.8);  poseSliders.LLOffsetY:SetValue(-1.2);  poseSliders.LLOffsetZ:SetValue(-0.8)
                poseSliders.RLOffsetX:SetValue(1);     poseSliders.RLOffsetY:SetValue(-1.2);  poseSliders.RLOffsetZ:SetValue(-0.85)
                poseSliders.HeadRotX:SetValue(-17);    poseSliders.HeadRotY:SetValue(0);      poseSliders.HeadRotZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.TorsoRotX:SetValue(0);     poseSliders.TorsoRotY:SetValue(0);     poseSliders.TorsoRotZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.LARotX:SetValue(69);       poseSliders.LARotY:SetValue(-25);      poseSliders.LARotZ:SetValue(34)
                poseSliders.RARotX:SetValue(45);       poseSliders.RARotY:SetValue(46);       poseSliders.RARotZ:SetValue(-23)
                poseSliders.LLRotX:SetValue(-99);      poseSliders.LLRotY:SetValue(180);      poseSliders.LLRotZ:SetValue(157)
                poseSliders.RLRotX:SetValue(-99);      poseSliders.RLRotY:SetValue(-180);     poseSliders.RLRotZ:SetValue(-153)
            elseif sel == "Penis" then
                poseSliders.TorsoOffsetX:SetValue(0);  poseSliders.TorsoOffsetY:SetValue(-0.8); poseSliders.TorsoOffsetZ:SetValue(-6.5)
                poseSliders.TorsoRotX:SetValue(-90);   poseSliders.TorsoRotY:SetValue(0);      poseSliders.TorsoRotZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.HeadOffsetX:SetValue(0);   poseSliders.HeadOffsetY:SetValue(1.5);  poseSliders.HeadOffsetZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.HeadRotX:SetValue(90);     poseSliders.HeadRotY:SetValue(0);       poseSliders.HeadRotZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.LAOffsetX:SetValue(0);     poseSliders.LAOffsetY:SetValue(-2);     poseSliders.LAOffsetZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.RAOffsetX:SetValue(0);     poseSliders.RAOffsetY:SetValue(-4);     poseSliders.RAOffsetZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.LLOffsetX:SetValue(-1);    poseSliders.LLOffsetY:SetValue(-5.5);   poseSliders.LLOffsetZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.LLRotX:SetValue(0);        poseSliders.LLRotY:SetValue(0);         poseSliders.LLRotZ:SetValue(-90)
                poseSliders.RLOffsetX:SetValue(1);     poseSliders.RLOffsetY:SetValue(-5.5);   poseSliders.RLOffsetZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.RLRotX:SetValue(0);        poseSliders.RLRotY:SetValue(0);         poseSliders.RLRotZ:SetValue(90)
                poseSliders.LARotX:SetValue(0);        poseSliders.LARotY:SetValue(0);         poseSliders.LARotZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.RARotX:SetValue(0);        poseSliders.RARotY:SetValue(0);         poseSliders.RARotZ:SetValue(0)
            elseif sel == "Blowjob" then
                poseSliders.HeadRotX:SetValue(-1);     poseSliders.HeadRotY:SetValue(0);       poseSliders.HeadRotZ:SetValue(9)
                poseSliders.TorsoRotX:SetValue(22);    poseSliders.TorsoRotY:SetValue(180);    poseSliders.TorsoRotZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.LARotX:SetValue(137);      poseSliders.LARotY:SetValue(-21);       poseSliders.LARotZ:SetValue(20)
                poseSliders.RARotX:SetValue(137);      poseSliders.RARotY:SetValue(3);         poseSliders.RARotZ:SetValue(-20)

                poseSliders.HeadOffsetX:SetValue(0);   poseSliders.HeadOffsetY:SetValue(1.5);  poseSliders.HeadOffsetZ:SetValue(0)
                poseSliders.TorsoOffsetX:SetValue(0);  poseSliders.TorsoOffsetY:SetValue(-1.9); poseSliders.TorsoOffsetZ:SetValue(-2)
                poseSliders.LAOffsetX:SetValue(-1.3);  poseSliders.LAOffsetY:SetValue(1.2);    poseSliders.LAOffsetZ:SetValue(-0.5)
                poseSliders.RAOffsetX:SetValue(1.3);   poseSliders.RAOffsetY:SetValue(1.1);    poseSliders.RAOffsetZ:SetValue(-0.4)
                poseSliders.LLOffsetX:SetValue(-0.8);  poseSliders.LLOffsetY:SetValue(-1.3);   poseSliders.LLOffsetZ:SetValue(0.8)
                poseSliders.RLOffsetX:SetValue(0.8);   poseSliders.RLOffsetY:SetValue(-1.2);   poseSliders.RLOffsetZ:SetValue(0.8)
            end

    })

    PosesTargetGroup:AddToggle("CloneOption", {
        Text = "Use Clone",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(arg)
            useClone = arg
        end,
    })

    -- ============================================================
    -- Apply Pose (original logic)
    -- ============================================================
    PosesTargetGroup:AddDivider("Apply")

    PosesTargetGroup:AddToggle("LoopPosePlayer", {
        Text = "Apply Pose",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(arg)
            if arg then
                loopActive = true
                task.spawn(function()
                    while loopActive and PosesRunService.RenderStepped:Wait() do
                        local humanoidRootPart = posesChar and posesChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local humanoid = posesChar and posesChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

                        if not useClone then
                            local PlayerForPosing = selectedPlayerForPoses
                            if PlayerForPosing then
                                targetCharacter = PlayerForPosing.Character
                            end
                        elseif posesToysFolder and posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("YouDecoy") and useClone then
                            targetCharacter = posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("YouDecoy")
                        else
                            ClyrixNotify("Nil'e", "Spawn Clone", 5)
                            if Toggles.LoopPosePlayer then Toggles.LoopPosePlayer:SetValue(false) end
                            return
                        end

                        if targetCharacter and humanoidRootPart and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and loopActive then
                            if not posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("RagdollForPoses") then
                                for _, v27 in posesToysFolder:GetChildren() do
                                    if v27.Name == "FoodPlate" then
                                        v27.Name = "idi nahui otsyuda"
                                        PosesDestroyT(v27)
                                    end
                                end

                                while true do
                                    PosesRunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                                    if not (PosesLocalPlayer.CanSpawnToy.Value and not PosesLocalPlayer.InPlot.Value) then
                                        continue
                                    end
                                    break
                                end

                                PosesSpawnToy("FoodPlate", humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 20))
                                foodPlate = posesToysFolder:WaitForChild("FoodPlate", 1)

                                if foodPlate then
                                    local soundPart = foodPlate:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 1)
                                    if soundPart then
                                        while true do
                                            PosesRunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                                            PosesSetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame)
                                            local flag9 = not foodPlate or not soundPart
                                            local partOwner
                                            if flag9 then
                                                partOwner = flag9
                                            else
                                                partOwner = soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                                if partOwner then
                                                    partOwner = soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner").Value == PosesLocalPlayer.Name
                                                end
                                            end
                                            if not (partOwner or not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not loopActive) then
                                                continue
                                            end
                                            break
                                        end

                                        local v27 = foodPlate
                                        local partOwner
                                        if foodPlate then partOwner = soundPart else partOwner = v27 end
                                        partOwner = partOwner and soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                        if partOwner then
                                            partOwner = soundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner").Value == PosesLocalPlayer.Name
                                        end

                                        if partOwner then
                                            soundPart.CanCollide = false
                                            soundPart.CanQuery = false
                                            soundPart.Transparency = 1
                                            soundPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 670000, 0)
                                            local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition", soundPart)
                                            bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                            bodyPosition.D = 100
                                            bodyPosition.Position = Vector3.new(0, 670000, 0)
                                            foodPlate.Name = "RagdollForPoses"

                                            task.spawn(function()
                                                local foodPlate2 = PosesLocalPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyDestroy
                                                    .Contents:WaitForChild("FoodPlate", 1)
                                                if foodPlate2 then
                                                    foodPlate2.Name = "For Poses"
                                                    foodPlate2.ViewItemButton.Season.Visible = true
                                                    foodPlate2.ViewItemButton.Season.Text = "For Poses"
                                                end
                                            end)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                local partOwner = posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("RagdollForPoses") and
                                    posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("RagdollForPoses"):FindFirstChild("SoundPart") and
                                    posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("RagdollForPoses"):FindFirstChild("SoundPart"):FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                if partOwner then
                                    partOwner = posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("RagdollForPoses"):FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                                        :FindFirstChild("PartOwner").Value == PosesLocalPlayer.Name
                                end

                                if partOwner then
                                    local humanoidRootPart2 = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    local humanoid2 = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                    local head = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head")

                                    if humanoidRootPart2 and humanoid2 and humanoid2.Health > 0 and head and loopActive then
                                        local cFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame

                                        if (humanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude > 25 and loopActive then
                                            while true do
                                                humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart2.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 1.5)
                                                humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                humanoidRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                                PosesRunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                                                PosesSetNetworkOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart2, humanoidRootPart2.CFrame)
                                                local flag9 = not targetCharacter or not humanoidRootPart2 or not humanoid2 or humanoid2.Health <= 0 or not head
                                                if not flag9 then
                                                    flag9 = head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                                    if flag9 then
                                                        flag9 = head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner").Value == PosesLocalPlayer.Name
                                                    end
                                                end
                                                if not (flag9 or not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not loopActive) then
                                                    continue
                                                end
                                                break
                                            end

                                            if humanoidRootPart then
                                                humanoidRootPart.CFrame = cFrame
                                                humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                humanoidRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                            end
                                            if loopActive and humanoidRootPart2 then
                                                humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = cFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5)
                                                humanoidRootPart2.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                humanoidRootPart2.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                            end
                                        end

                                        if loopActive and humanoidRootPart and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoidRootPart2 and humanoid2 and humanoid2.Health > 0 then
                                            local partOwner2 = loopActive and head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                                            if partOwner2 then
                                                partOwner2 = head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner").Value == PosesLocalPlayer.Name
                                            end

                                            if partOwner2 then
                                                task.spawn(function()
                                                    pcall(function()
                                                        if humanoid2:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") and not humanoid2:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled").Value then
                                                            task.wait(0.05)
                                                            posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("RagdollForPoses"):FindFirstChild("SoundPart").CFrame =
                                                                targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso").CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0) *
                                                                CFrame.Angles(1.5707963267948966, 0, 0)
                                                            task.wait(0.1)
                                                            posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("RagdollForPoses"):FindFirstChild("SoundPart").CFrame =
                                                                CFrame.new(0, 670000, 0)
                                                        end
                                                    end)
                                                    pcall(function()
                                                        humanoidRootPart2.CanCollide = false
                                                        head.CanCollide = false
                                                        targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso").CanCollide = false
                                                        targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Left Arm"):FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart").CanCollide = false
                                                        targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Right Arm"):FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart").CanCollide = false
                                                        targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Left Leg"):FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart").CanCollide = false
                                                        targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Right Leg"):FindFirstChild("RagdollLimbPart").CanCollide = false
                                                    end)
                                                end)

                                                pcall(function()
                                                    targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso").CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame *
                                                        CFrame.new(n16, n17, n18) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(n34), math.rad(n35), math.rad(n36))
                                                    head.CFrame = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso").CFrame *
                                                        CFrame.new(n13, n14, n15) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(n31), math.rad(n32), math.rad(n33))
                                                    targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Left Arm").CFrame = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso").CFrame *
                                                        CFrame.new(n19, n20, n21) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(n37), math.rad(n38), math.rad(n39))
                                                    targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Right Arm").CFrame = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso").CFrame *
                                                        CFrame.new(n22, n23, n24) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(n40), math.rad(n41), math.rad(n42))
                                                    targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Left Leg").CFrame = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso").CFrame *
                                                        CFrame.new(n25, n26, n27) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(n43), math.rad(n44), math.rad(n45))
                                                    targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Right Leg").CFrame = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso").CFrame *
                                                        CFrame.new(n28, n29, n30) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(n46), math.rad(n47), math.rad(n48))
                                                end)

                                                pcall(function()
                                                    humanoidRootPart2.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                    targetCharacter["Left Arm"].Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                    targetCharacter["Right Arm"].Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                    targetCharacter["Left Leg"].Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                    targetCharacter["Right Leg"].Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                                end)
                                            else
                                                PosesSetNetworkOwner:FireServer(humanoidRootPart2, humanoidRootPart2.CFrame)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                else
                                    PosesDestroyT(posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("RagdollForPoses"))
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            else
                loopActive = false

                if not useClone then
                    local PlayerForPosing = selectedPlayerForPoses
                    if PlayerForPosing then
                        targetCharacter = PlayerForPosing.Character
                    end
                elseif posesToysFolder and posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("YouDecoy") and useClone then
                    targetCharacter = posesToysFolder:FindFirstChild("YouDecoy")
                end

                if targetCharacter then
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    pcall(function()
                        PosesGrabEvents.DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                        targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CanCollide = true
                    end)
                end
            end
        end,
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(3) do
            if Options.SelectPosePlayer then
                pcall(function() Options.SelectPosePlayer:SetValues(PosesGetPlayerList()) end)
            end
        end
    end)
end
InitPosesTab()
