                SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://155657655", SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://155674246",
                SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://155657609", SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://155657671",
                SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://155657619", SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://155674931",
                ClockTime = 14, Brightness = 2.5,
                FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 240, 200), FogStart = 0, FogEnd = 2500,
                Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(140, 220, 170), OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(180, 240, 200),
                CCTint = Color3.fromRGB(200, 255, 220), CCSaturation = 0.5, CCContrast = 0.15, CCBrightness = 0.08,
                AtmosDensity = 0.35, AtmosOffset = 0.15, AtmosColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 255, 200),
                AtmosDecay = Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 120), AtmosGlare = 0.15, AtmosHaze = 1.5,
            },
            ["Midnight Purple"] = {
                SkyboxBk = "rbxassetid://135207744", SkyboxDn = "rbxassetid://135207662",
                SkyboxFt = "rbxassetid://135207770", SkyboxLf = "rbxassetid://135207615",
                SkyboxRt = "rbxassetid://135207695", SkyboxUp = "rbxassetid://135207794",
                ClockTime = 0, Brightness = 1.2,
                FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(25, 15, 50), FogStart = 0, FogEnd = 3000,
                Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(50, 30, 80), OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(70, 45, 110),
                CCTint = Color3.fromRGB(180, 140, 255), CCSaturation = 0.4, CCContrast = 0.3, CCBrightness = -0.1,
                AtmosDensity = 0.35, AtmosOffset = 0.2, AtmosColor = Color3.fromRGB(100, 60, 180),
                AtmosDecay = Color3.fromRGB(50, 30, 100), AtmosGlare = 0.3, AtmosHaze = 1.8,
            },
        }

        local skyEnabled = false
        local skyCurrentSky, skyCurrentCC, skyCurrentAtmosCustom = nil, nil, nil
        local skyActivePresetName = nil
        local skyPendingPreset = nil
        local skyOriginal = nil
        local skyEnforceConn = nil

        local function skySnapshotOriginal()
            if skyOriginal then return end
            skyOriginal = {
                Brightness = LightingSvc.Brightness, ClockTime = LightingSvc.ClockTime,
                FogColor = LightingSvc.FogColor, FogStart = LightingSvc.FogStart, FogEnd = LightingSvc.FogEnd,
                Ambient = LightingSvc.Ambient, OutdoorAmbient = LightingSvc.OutdoorAmbient,
            }
            local atmos = LightingSvc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
            if atmos then
                skyOriginal.AtmosRef = atmos
                skyOriginal.AtmosDensity = atmos.Density
                skyOriginal.AtmosOffset = atmos.Offset
                skyOriginal.AtmosColor = atmos.Color
                skyOriginal.AtmosDecay = atmos.Decay
                skyOriginal.AtmosGlare = atmos.Glare
                skyOriginal.AtmosHaze = atmos.Haze
            end
        end

        local function skyDestroyCustom()
            if skyCurrentSky then pcall(function() skyCurrentSky:Destroy() end) skyCurrentSky = nil end
            if skyCurrentCC then pcall(function() skyCurrentCC:Destroy() end) skyCurrentCC = nil end
            if skyCurrentAtmosCustom then pcall(function() skyCurrentAtmosCustom:Destroy() end) skyCurrentAtmosCustom = nil end
        end

        local function skyApplyPreset(name)
            local p = SkyPresets[name]
            if not p then return end
            skyActivePresetName = name
            skySnapshotOriginal()
            skyDestroyCustom()

            local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
            sky.Name = "_SkyChanger"
            sky.SkyboxBk = p.SkyboxBk; sky.SkyboxDn = p.SkyboxDn; sky.SkyboxFt = p.SkyboxFt
            sky.SkyboxLf = p.SkyboxLf; sky.SkyboxRt = p.SkyboxRt; sky.SkyboxUp = p.SkyboxUp
            sky.SunAngularSize = 12; sky.MoonAngularSize = 10; sky.StarCount = 3000
            sky.Parent = LightingSvc
            skyCurrentSky = sky

            local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            cc.Name = "_SkyCC"
            cc.TintColor = p.CCTint; cc.Saturation = p.CCSaturation
            cc.Contrast = p.CCContrast; cc.Brightness = p.CCBrightness
            cc.Parent = LightingSvc
            skyCurrentCC = cc

            local atmos = LightingSvc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
            if atmos then
                atmos.Density = p.AtmosDensity; atmos.Offset = p.AtmosOffset
                atmos.Color = p.AtmosColor; atmos.Decay = p.AtmosDecay
                atmos.Glare = p.AtmosGlare; atmos.Haze = p.AtmosHaze
            else
                local a = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
                a.Name = "_SkyAtmos"
                a.Density = p.AtmosDensity; a.Offset = p.AtmosOffset
                a.Color = p.AtmosColor; a.Decay = p.AtmosDecay
                a.Glare = p.AtmosGlare; a.Haze = p.AtmosHaze
                a.Parent = LightingSvc
                skyCurrentAtmosCustom = a
            end
        end

        local function skyStartEnforce()
            if skyEnforceConn then skyEnforceConn:Disconnect() end
            skyEnforceConn = RService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not skyEnabled or not skyActivePresetName then return end
                local p = SkyPresets[skyActivePresetName]
                if not p then return end
                pcall(function()
                    for _, inst in ipairs(LightingSvc:GetChildren()) do
                        if inst:IsA("Sky") and inst ~= skyCurrentSky and inst.Enabled then
                            inst.Enabled = false
                        end
                    end
                    if skyCurrentSky and skyCurrentSky.Parent ~= LightingSvc then skyCurrentSky.Parent = LightingSvc end
                    if skyCurrentCC and skyCurrentCC.Parent ~= LightingSvc then skyCurrentCC.Parent = LightingSvc end
                    LightingSvc.Brightness = p.Brightness
                    LightingSvc.ClockTime = p.ClockTime
                    LightingSvc.FogColor = p.FogColor
                    LightingSvc.FogStart = p.FogStart
                    LightingSvc.FogEnd = p.FogEnd
                    LightingSvc.Ambient = p.Ambient
                    LightingSvc.OutdoorAmbient = p.OutdoorAmbient
                end)
            end)
        end

        local function skyStopEnforce()
            if skyEnforceConn then skyEnforceConn:Disconnect() skyEnforceConn = nil end
        end

        local function skyRestoreOriginal()
            skyStopEnforce()
            skyDestroyCustom()
            if skyOriginal then
                pcall(function()
                    LightingSvc.Brightness = skyOriginal.Brightness
                    LightingSvc.ClockTime = skyOriginal.ClockTime
                    LightingSvc.FogColor = skyOriginal.FogColor
                    LightingSvc.FogStart = skyOriginal.FogStart
                    LightingSvc.FogEnd = skyOriginal.FogEnd
                    LightingSvc.Ambient = skyOriginal.Ambient
                    LightingSvc.OutdoorAmbient = skyOriginal.OutdoorAmbient
                end)
                if skyOriginal.AtmosRef and skyOriginal.AtmosRef.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        skyOriginal.AtmosRef.Density = skyOriginal.AtmosDensity
                        skyOriginal.AtmosRef.Offset = skyOriginal.AtmosOffset
                        skyOriginal.AtmosRef.Color = skyOriginal.AtmosColor
                        skyOriginal.AtmosRef.Decay = skyOriginal.AtmosDecay
                        skyOriginal.AtmosRef.Glare = skyOriginal.AtmosGlare
                        skyOriginal.AtmosRef.Haze = skyOriginal.AtmosHaze
                    end)
                end
            end
            for _, inst in ipairs(LightingSvc:GetChildren()) do
                if inst:IsA("Sky") then inst.Enabled = true end
            end
            skyActivePresetName = nil
        end

        -- Sky UI
        ShaderGroup:AddDivider("Sky Shader")

        ShaderGroup:AddToggle("SkyShaderToggle", {
            Text = "Sky Shader",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                skyEnabled = v
                if v then
                    if skyPendingPreset and SkyPresets[skyPendingPreset] then
                        skyApplyPreset(skyPendingPreset)
                    elseif skyActivePresetName and SkyPresets[skyActivePresetName] then
                        skyApplyPreset(skyActivePresetName)
                    else
                        skyApplyPreset("Rainy")
                        skyPendingPreset = "Rainy"
                    end
                    skyStartEnforce()
                    ClyrixNotify("Sky", "Sky Shader ON", 2)
                else
                    skyRestoreOriginal()
                    ClyrixNotify("Sky", "Sky Shader OFF", 2)
                end
            end,
        })

        local skyPresetNames = {}
        for presetName in pairs(SkyPresets) do table.insert(skyPresetNames, presetName) end
        table.sort(skyPresetNames)

        ShaderGroup:AddDropdown("SkyPresetSelect", {
            Text = "Sky Preset",
            Values = skyPresetNames,
            Default = "Rainy",
            Callback = function(Value)
                pcall(function()
                    local name = Value
                    if type(name) ~= "string" then return end
                    if not SkyPresets[name] then return end
                    if skyEnabled then
                        skyApplyPreset(name)
                    else
                        skyPendingPreset = name
                    end
                end)
            end,
        })

        ShaderGroup:AddButton({
            Text = "Restore Original Sky",
            Func = function()
                skyRestoreOriginal()
                skyPendingPreset = nil
                ClyrixNotify("Sky", "Original sky restored", 2)
            end,
        })
    end
    SkyInit()

    -- ============================================================
    -- Snow Shader
    -- ============================================================
    local function SnowInit()
        local SnowWorkspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local SnowRService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local SnowPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

        local snowSettings = {
            rate = 500, speed = 35, size = 0.6, spread = 20,
            opacity = 0, color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            height = 30, areaSize = 220,
        }

        local SNOW_TEXTURE = "rbxassetid://118641183"

        local snowFolder = nil
        local snowPart = nil
        local snowEmitter = nil
        local snowFollowConnection = nil
        local snowEnabled = false

        local function snowDestroy()
            if snowFollowConnection then
                snowFollowConnection:Disconnect()
                snowFollowConnection = nil
            end
            if snowFolder then
                pcall(function() snowFolder:Destroy() end)
                snowFolder = nil
            end
            snowPart = nil
            snowEmitter = nil
        end

        local function snowCreate()
            snowDestroy()
            snowFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
            snowFolder.Name = "_SnowFX"
            snowFolder.Parent = SnowWorkspace

            snowPart = Instance.new("Part")
            snowPart.Name = "SnowEmitter"
            snowPart.Size = Vector3.new(snowSettings.areaSize, 1, snowSettings.areaSize)
            snowPart.Transparency = 1
            snowPart.Anchored = true
            snowPart.CanCollide = false
            snowPart.CanQuery = false
            snowPart.CanTouch = false
            snowPart.CastShadow = false
            snowPart.Parent = snowFolder

            snowEmitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
            snowEmitter.Name = "SnowFlake"
            snowEmitter.Texture = SNOW_TEXTURE
            snowEmitter.Rate = snowSettings.rate
            snowEmitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(4, 8)
            snowEmitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(snowSettings.speed * 0.5, snowSettings.speed)
            snowEmitter.Drag = 0.3
            snowEmitter.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, -snowSettings.speed * 0.4, 0)
            snowEmitter.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
            snowEmitter.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-25, 25)
            snowEmitter.Size = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, snowSettings.size * 0.5),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, snowSettings.size),
            })
            snowEmitter.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, snowSettings.opacity),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, snowSettings.opacity),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
            })
            snowEmitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(snowSettings.color)
            snowEmitter.LightEmission = 0.1
            snowEmitter.LightInfluence = 0
            snowEmitter.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
            snowEmitter.ShapeStyle = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume
            snowEmitter.ShapeInOut = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Outward
            snowEmitter.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
            snowEmitter.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(snowSettings.spread, snowSettings.spread)
            snowEmitter.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel
            snowEmitter.Parent = snowPart

            snowFollowConnection = SnowRService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                local char = SnowPlayer.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then return end
                snowPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, snowSettings.height, 0))
            end)
        end

        local function snowUpdateSize()
            if not snowEmitter then return end
            snowEmitter.Size = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, snowSettings.size * 0.5),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, snowSettings.size),
            })
        end

        local function snowSetEnabled(state)
            snowEnabled = state
            if state then
                snowCreate()
                ClyrixNotify("Snow", "Snowfall enabled", 2)
            else
                snowDestroy()
                ClyrixNotify("Snow", "Snowfall disabled", 2)
            end
        end

        ShaderGroup:AddDivider("Snow Shader")

        ShaderGroup:AddToggle("SnowShaderToggle", {
            Text = "Snow Shader",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(v) snowSetEnabled(v) end,
        })

        ShaderGroup:AddSlider("SnowSizeSlider", {
            Text = "Snow Size",
            Default = 0.6,
            Min = 0.1,
            Max = 3,
            Rounding = 1,
            Callback = function(v)
                snowSettings.size = v
                if snowEnabled then snowUpdateSize() end
            end,
        })

        SnowPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            if snowEnabled then
                task.wait(0.5)
                if snowEnabled then snowCreate() end
            end
        end)
    end
    SnowInit()
end
InitShaderTab()
-- =====================================================
-- Grab タブ: grab.txt 統合（完全版）
-- =====================================================
function InitGrabTab()
    local GrabPlayers = game:GetService("Players")
    local GrabReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local GrabRunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local GrabWorkspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local GrabUserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local GrabDebris = game:GetService("Debris")
    local GrabLocalPlayer = GrabPlayers.LocalPlayer
    local GrabCamera = GrabWorkspace.CurrentCamera

    -- ===== 状態テーブル =====
    local grabModes = {
        ["Kill Grab"] = false,
        ["Sky Grab"] = false,
        ["Spin Grab"] = false,
        ["Teleport Up"] = false,
        ["Teleport Down"] = false,
        ["Chaos Grab"] = false,
        ["Supersonic Grab"] = false,
        ["Freeze Grab"] = false,
    }

    local grabCfg = {
        SuperStrength = false,
        Strength = 5000,
        lineColor1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
        lineColor2 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255),
        lineColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),
        lineColor4 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0),
        lineColor5 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255),
        lineColor6 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),
        IncreaseLineExtend = 3,
    }

    -- =====================================================
    -- Grab ハンドラ関数群（ソースそのまま）
    -- =====================================================
    local function handleKillGrab(arg)
        pcall(function()
            local grabPart_ = arg:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
            local weldConstraint = grabPart_ and grabPart_:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
            if weldConstraint and weldConstraint.Part1 then
                weldConstraint.Part1.Parent:BreakJoints()
            end
        end)
    end

    local function handleSkyGrab(arg)
        pcall(function()
            local grabPart_ = arg:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
            if grabPart_ and grabPart_:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint") then
                local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)
                bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
                bodyVelocity.Parent = grabPart_
            end
        end)
    end

    local function handleSpinGrab(arg)
        pcall(function()
            local grabPart_ = arg:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
            grabPart_ = grabPart_ and grabPart_:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
            if grabPart_ and grabPart_.Part1 then
                local bodyAngularVelocity = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
                bodyAngularVelocity.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)
                bodyAngularVelocity.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
                bodyAngularVelocity.Parent = grabPart_.Part1
                for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
                    child:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function handleChaosGrab(arg, isActiveFn)
        pcall(function()
            local grabPart_ = arg:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
            if not grabPart_ then return end
            local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
            bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
            bodyPosition.Parent = grabPart_
            local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
            bodyGyro.Parent = grabPart_
            task.spawn(function()
                while isActiveFn() do
                    pcall(function()
                        local random = math.random
                        bodyPosition.Position = grabPart_.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-50, 50), math.random(-50, 50), random(-50, 50))
                        local rad = math.rad
                        local random2 = math.random
                        bodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(math.random(-180, 180)), math.rad(math.random(-180, 180)), rad(random2(-180, 180)))
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
                bodyPosition:Destroy()
                bodyGyro:Destroy()
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function handleSupersonicGrab(arg)
        pcall(function()
            local grabPart_ = arg:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
            if grabPart_ then
                local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 350, 0)
                bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
                bodyVelocity.Parent = grabPart_
            end
        end)
    end

    local function handleFreezeGrab(arg)
        pcall(function()
            local grabPart_ = arg:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
            if grabPart_ then
                local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                bodyPosition.Position = grabPart_.Position
                bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                bodyPosition.Parent = grabPart_
            end
        end)
    end

    local function handleTeleport(arg, dir)
        pcall(function()
            local grabPart_ = arg:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
            if grabPart_ then
                grabPart_.Position = grabPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, dir == "up" and 30 or -30, 0)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- =====================================================
    -- GrabParts ハンドラ（UI作成より先に接続）
    -- =====================================================
    GrabWorkspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name ~= "GrabParts" then return end

        if grabModes["Kill Grab"] then handleKillGrab(child) end
        if grabModes["Sky Grab"] then handleSkyGrab(child) end
        if grabModes["Spin Grab"] then handleSpinGrab(child) end
        if grabModes["Teleport Up"] then handleTeleport(child, "up") end
        if grabModes["Teleport Down"] then handleTeleport(child, "down") end
        if grabModes["Chaos Grab"] then
            handleChaosGrab(child, function() return grabModes["Chaos Grab"] end)
        end
        if grabModes["Supersonic Grab"] then handleSupersonicGrab(child) end
        if grabModes["Freeze Grab"] then handleFreezeGrab(child) end

        if grabCfg.SuperStrength then
            task.spawn(function()
                local grabPart_ = child:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 5)
                if not grabPart_ or not grabPart_:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint") then return end
                local part1 = grabPart_.WeldConstraint.Part1
                if not part1 then return end
                local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", part1)
                bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.zero
                bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new()
                bodyVelocity.Name = "SuperStrength"
                local conn
                conn = child:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
                    if child.Parent then return end
                    local lastInput = GrabUserInputService:GetLastInputType()
                    local isRelease = (lastInput ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and not _G.MobileSuperStrengthTriggered) or not grabCfg.SuperStrength
                    if isRelease then
                        local isClick = (lastInput == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) or (lastInput == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
                        if isClick then
                            bodyVelocity:Destroy()
                        end
                    else
                        bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        bodyVelocity.Velocity = GrabWorkspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.lookVector * (grabCfg.Strength or 5000)
                        GrabDebris:AddItem(bodyVelocity, 1)
                    end
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end)
            end)
        end
    end)

    -- =====================================================
    -- UI: 左側 Grab Mode
    -- =====================================================
    local GrabModeGroup = Tabs.Grab:AddLeftGroupbox("Grab Mode", "hand")

    for modeName in pairs(grabModes) do
        GrabModeGroup:AddToggle("GrabMode_" .. modeName:gsub("%s", "_"), {
            Text = modeName,
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                grabModes[modeName] = v
            end,
        })
    end

    -- =====================================================
    -- UI: 左側 Power and Gamepass
    -- =====================================================
    local PowerGroup = Tabs.Grab:AddLeftGroupbox("Power and Gamepass", "zap")

    -- Super Strength モバイルボタン
    local superStrengthButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    superStrengthButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    superStrengthButton.Text = ""
    superStrengthButton.Active = false
    superStrengthButton.Visible = false

    task.spawn(function()
        local playerGui = GrabLocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
        playerGui = playerGui and playerGui:WaitForChild("ContextActionGui", 5)
        playerGui = playerGui and playerGui:WaitForChild("ContextButtonFrame", 5)
        if not playerGui then return end
        local conn
        local function checkAndMove(obj)
            if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") and obj.Image == "rbxassetid://9603669631" then
                if obj.Parent and obj.Parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
                    superStrengthButton.Parent = obj.Parent
                    superStrengthButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                    superStrengthButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do
            if checkAndMove(d) then return end
        end
        conn = playerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if checkAndMove(d) and conn then conn:Disconnect() end
        end)
    end)

    superStrengthButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            if superStrengthButton.Visible then
                _G.MobileSuperStrengthTriggered = true
                task.delay(0.5, function()
                    _G.MobileSuperStrengthTriggered = false
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    PowerGroup:AddToggle("SuperStrengthGrab", {
        Text = "Super Strength",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            grabCfg.SuperStrength = v
            superStrengthButton.Visible = v
        end,
    })

    PowerGroup:AddSlider("StrengthValue", {
        Text = "Strength",
        Default = 5000,
        Min = 10,
        Max = 10000,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v)
            grabCfg.Strength = v
        end,
    })

    PowerGroup:AddDivider("Gamepass")

    PowerGroup:AddToggle("FreeGamepassReach", {
        Text = "Further Reach",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            local fartherReach = GrabLocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("FartherReach")
            if v then
                if not fartherReach then
                    fartherReach = Instance.new("BoolValue")
                    fartherReach.Name = "FartherReach"
                    fartherReach.Parent = GrabLocalPlayer
                end
                fartherReach.Value = true
                local notifier = GrabReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GamepassEvents")
                notifier = notifier and notifier:FindFirstChild("FurtherReachBoughtNotifier")
                if notifier then
                    for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(notifier.OnClientEvent)) do
                        pcall(c.Function)
                    end
                end
            elseif fartherReach then
                fartherReach:Destroy()
            end
        end,
    })

    -- =====================================================
    -- Line Extend モバイルボタン制御
    -- =====================================================
    local extendButtonState = nil
    do
        local lineExtendDistance = 3
        local holdActive = false
        local grabbingScript = nil
        local grabbingEnv = nil
        local grabPartsRef = nil
        local camDist = 0

        local extendScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        extendScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        extendScreenGui.Name = "FurtherExtendGUI"
        if GrabUserInputService.TouchEnabled then
            extendScreenGui.Parent = GrabLocalPlayer.PlayerGui
        end

        local extendUpBtn = Instance.new("ImageButton")
        extendUpBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
        extendUpBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, -259)
        extendUpBtn.Image = "rbxassetid://97166444"
        extendUpBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        extendUpBtn.ImageTransparency = 1
        extendUpBtn.Visible = false
        extendUpBtn.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
        extendUpBtn.Parent = extendScreenGui

        local extendUpIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        extendUpIcon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        extendUpIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://9603831913"
        extendUpIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        extendUpIcon.Visible = false
        extendUpIcon.Parent = extendUpBtn

        local extendDownBtn = Instance.new("ImageButton")
        extendDownBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
        extendDownBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, -211)
        extendDownBtn.Image = "rbxassetid://97166444"
        extendDownBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        extendDownBtn.ImageTransparency = 1
        extendDownBtn.Visible = false
        extendDownBtn.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
        extendDownBtn.Parent = extendScreenGui

        local extendDownIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        extendDownIcon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        extendDownIcon.Image = "rbxassetid://9603826756"
        extendDownIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        extendDownIcon.Visible = false
        extendDownIcon.Parent = extendDownBtn

        local function setupAutoPosition(btn, iconImage)
            task.spawn(function()
                local pg = GrabLocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
                pg = pg and pg:WaitForChild("ContextActionGui", 5)
                pg = pg and pg:WaitForChild("ContextButtonFrame", 5)
                if not pg then return end
                local conn
                local function check(obj)
                    if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") and obj.Image == iconImage then
                        if obj.Parent and obj.Parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
                            btn.Parent = obj.Parent
                            btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                            btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                    return false
                end
                for _, d in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
                    if check(d) then return end
                end
                conn = pg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                    if check(d) and conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                end)
            end)
        end

        setupAutoPosition(extendUpBtn, "rbxassetid://9603831913")
        setupAutoPosition(extendDownBtn, "rbxassetid://9603826756")

        extendButtonState = function(state)
            local on = state and _G.FurtherExtend
            extendUpBtn.Visible = on
            extendUpBtn.Active = on
            extendDownBtn.Visible = on
            extendDownBtn.Active = on
        end

        local function btnIncrease()
            if grabbingEnv and grabbingEnv.distance and _G.FurtherExtend then
                grabbingEnv.distance = (grabbingEnv.distance or 0) + grabCfg.IncreaseLineExtend
                if grabbingEnv.distance < lineExtendDistance then
                    grabbingEnv.distance = lineExtendDistance
                end
            end
        end

        local function btnDecrease()
            if grabbingEnv and grabbingEnv.distance and _G.FurtherExtend then
                grabbingEnv.distance = (grabbingEnv.distance or 0) - grabCfg.IncreaseLineExtend
                if grabbingEnv.distance < lineExtendDistance then
                    grabbingEnv.distance = lineExtendDistance
                end
            end
        end

        GrabWorkspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "GrabParts" and child:IsA("Model") and _G.FurtherExtend then
                if GrabUserInputService.MouseEnabled then
                    grabPartsRef = child
                    pcall(function()
                        grabPartsRef:WaitForChild("GrabPart")
                        grabPartsRef:WaitForChild("DragPart")
                        local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition", grabPartsRef.GrabPart)
                        bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(275000, 275000, 275000)
                        bp.P = 20000
                        bp.D = 950
                        bp.Position = grabPartsRef.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1.Position
                        camDist = (grabPartsRef.GrabPart.Position - GrabCamera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                        grabPartsRef.DragPart.AlignPosition.Enabled = false
                        task.spawn(function()
                            while grabPartsRef.Parent do
                                bp.Position = GrabCamera.CFrame.Position + GrabCamera.CFrame.LookVector * camDist
                                task.wait()
                            end
                            camDist = 0
                            bp:Destroy()
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                extendButtonState(true)
            end
        end)

        GrabWorkspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "GrabParts" and child:IsA("Model") then
                extendButtonState(false)
            end
        end)

        GrabUserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel and _G.FurtherExtend then
                if camDist < 11 then camDist = 11 end
                if input.Position.Z <= 0 then
                    if input.Position.Z < 0 then camDist -= grabCfg.IncreaseLineExtend end
                else
                    camDist += grabCfg.IncreaseLineExtend
                end
            end
        end)

        local function continuousIncrease()
            while holdActive do
                btnIncrease()
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end
        local function continuousDecrease()
            while holdActive do
                btnDecrease()
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end

        extendUpBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
            if not processed and GrabUserInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                holdActive = true
                task.spawn(continuousIncrease)
            end
        end)
        extendUpBtn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if GrabUserInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                holdActive = false
            end
        end)
        extendDownBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
            if not processed and GrabUserInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                holdActive = true
                task.spawn(continuousDecrease)
            end
        end)
        extendDownBtn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if GrabUserInputService.TouchEnabled and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                holdActive = false
            end
        end)

        local function reconnectSenv()
            local character = GrabLocalPlayer.Character or GrabLocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
            character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            grabbingScript = character:WaitForChild("GrabbingScript", 5)
            if grabbingScript and getsenv then
                grabbingEnv = getsenv(grabbingScript)
            end
        end
        GrabLocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(reconnectSenv)
        task.spawn(reconnectSenv)
    end

    PowerGroup:AddDivider("Line Extend")

    PowerGroup:AddToggle("InfiniteLineExtend", {
        Text = "Infinity Extend Line",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            _G.FurtherExtend = v
            if extendButtonState then extendButtonState(v) end
        end,
    })

    PowerGroup:AddSlider("ExtendSpeed", {
        Text = "Speed",
        Default = 3,
        Min = 3,
        Max = 25,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v)
            grabCfg.IncreaseLineExtend = v
        end,
    })

    -- =====================================================
    -- UI: 右側 Line Color（Hex入力）
    -- =====================================================
    local LineColorGroup = Tabs.Grab:AddRightGroupbox("Line Color", "droplet")

    LineColorGroup:AddDivider("Line Colors")

    local function hexToColor(hex)
        hex = tostring(hex or ""):gsub("#", ""):gsub("%s", "")
        if #hex ~= 6 then return nil end
        local r = tonumber(hex:sub(1, 2), 16) or 0
        local g = tonumber(hex:sub(3, 4), 16) or 0
        local b = tonumber(hex:sub(5, 6), 16) or 0
        return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b)
    end

    local defaultHex = {"FF0000", "0000FF", "00FF00", "FFFF00", "FF00FF", "00FFFF"}

    for i = 1, 6 do
        local idx = i
        LineColorGroup:AddInput("LineColorHex" .. idx, {
            Text = "Line Color " .. idx .. " (Hex)",
            Default = defaultHex[idx],
            Placeholder = "#" .. defaultHex[idx],
            Callback = function(v)
                local c = hexToColor(v)
                if c then
                    grabCfg["lineColor" .. idx] = c
                end
            end,
        })
    end

    LineColorGroup:AddButton({
        Text = "Apply Line Color",
        Func = function()
            local points = {}
            for i = 1, 6 do
                table.insert(points, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((i - 1) / 5, grabCfg["lineColor" .. i]))
            end
            pcall(function()
                GrabReplicatedStorage.DataEvents.UpdateLineColorsEvent:FireServer(ColorSequence.new(points))
            end)
        end,
    })
end
InitGrabTab()

-- =====================================================
-- UI Settings
-- =====================================================
ThemeManager:SetLibrary(Library)
SaveManager:SetLibrary(Library)
SaveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings()
ThemeManager:SetFolder("Clyrix")
SaveManager:SetFolder("Clyrix/Config")
SaveManager:SetSubFolder("game-config")
ThemeManager:ApplyToTab(Tabs.UISettings)
SaveManager:BuildConfigSection(Tabs.UISettings)
SaveManager:LoadAutoloadConfig()
